import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import path from 'path';
import {defineConfig} from 'vite';

export default defineConfig(() => {
  return {
    plugins: [react(), tailwindcss()],
    resolve: {
      alias: {
        '@': path.resolve(__dirname, '.'),
      },
    },
    server: {
      // HMR is disabled in AI Studio via DISABLE_HMR env var.
      // Do not modify—file watching is disabled to prevent flickering during agent edits.
      hmr: process.env.DISABLE_HMR !== 'true',
      // Disable file watching when DISABLE_HMR is true to save CPU during agent edits.
      watch: process.env.DISABLE_HMR === 'true' ? null : {},
    },
  };
});

================================================================================
FILE: Dockerfile
================================================================================
# Stage 1: Build the React + Vite application
FROM node:20-alpine AS builder
WORKDIR /app
COPY package*.json ./
RUN npm install --legacy-peer-deps
COPY . .
RUN npm run build

# Stage 2: Serve with lightweight Nginx
FROM nginx:alpine
COPY --from=builder /app/dist /usr/share/nginx/html
RUN printf 'server {\n\
    listen 80;\n\
    server_name _;\n\
    root /usr/share/nginx/html;\n\
    index index.html;\n\
    gzip on;\n\
    gzip_types text/plain text/css application/json application/javascript text/xml application/xml application/xml+rss text/javascript image/svg+xml;\n\
    location /assets/ {\n\
        expires 30d;\n\
        add_header Cache-Control "public, no-transform";\n\
    }\n\
    location / {\n\
        try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;\n\
    }\n\
}\n' > /etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf
EXPOSE 80
CMD ["nginx", "-g", "daemon off;"]


================================================================================
FILE: auto-deploy.sh
================================================================================
#!/bin/bash
# SZGH Pakistan - 100% Hands-Free Auto-Updater
# Checks GitHub for new commits and rebuilds the container only when changes are detected.

REPO_DIR="/root/SZGH-WEB-DEV"
cd "$REPO_DIR" || exit 1

# Fetch latest info from GitHub without modifying files yet
git fetch origin main >/dev/null 2>&1 || git fetch origin master >/dev/null 2>&1

LOCAL=$(git rev-parse HEAD)
REMOTE=$(git rev-parse @{u})

if [ "$LOCAL" != "$REMOTE" ]; then
    echo "[$(date)] New update found on GitHub! Pulling and rebuilding szghpakistan.com..."
    git reset --hard "$REMOTE"
    sed -i 's/RUN npm install$/RUN npm install --legacy-peer-deps/' Dockerfile
    docker build -t szghpakistan-web .
    docker rm -f szgh
    docker run -d --name szgh --restart always --net proxy-network -p 8086:80 szghpakistan-web
    echo "[$(date)] Live website updated successfully!"
fi


================================================================================
FILE: nginx-szghpakistan.conf
================================================================================
server {
    listen 80;
    listen [::]:80;
    server_name szghpakistan.com www.szghpakistan.com;

    root /var/www/szghpakistan/dist;
    index index.html;

    # Gzip compression for fast loading
    gzip on;
    gzip_types text/plain text/css application/json application/javascript text/xml application/xml application/xml+rss text/javascript image/svg+xml;

    # Cache static assets (images, JS, CSS)
    location /assets/ {
        expires 30d;
        add_header Cache-Control "public, no-transform";
    }

    # Single-Page Application (SPA) routing
    location / {
        try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: firestore.rules
================================================================================
rules_version = '2';

service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {
    // Pillar 1 & Phase 4.1: Global Default-Deny Safety Net
    match /{document=**} {
      allow read, write: if false;
    }

    // Phase 3: Hardened Global Helpers
    function isValidId(id) {
      return id is string && id.size() >= 1 && id.size() <= 128 && id.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$');
    }

    function incoming() {
      return request.resource.data;
    }

    function existing() {
      return resource.data;
    }

    function isSignedIn() {
      return request.auth != null;
    }

    function isVerifiedUser() {
      return isSignedIn() && request.auth.token.email_verified == true;
    }

    function isAdmin() {
      return isVerifiedUser() && (
        request.auth.token.email in ['khubaib4000@gmail.com', 'grnoman786@gmail.com'] ||
        exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/admins/$(request.auth.uid))
      );
    }

    // Pillar 2 & Pillar 5: Validation Blueprint for InventoryItem (with 4-image gallery & defaultImageIndex)
    function isValidInventoryItem(data, docId) {
      return data.keys().hasAll([
          'productId',
          'modelNumber',
          'title',
          'category',
          'subcategory',
          'description',
          'price',
          'stockCount',
          'stockStatus',
          'defaultImageIndex',
          'images',
          'updatedBy',
          'updatedAt'
        ]) &&
        data.keys().hasOnly([
          'productId',
          'modelNumber',
          'title',
          'category',
          'subcategory',
          'description',
          'price',
          'stockCount',
          'stockStatus',
          'defaultImageIndex',
          'images',
          'updatedBy',
          'updatedAt'
        ]) &&
        data.productId is string &&
        data.productId == docId &&
        data.productId.size() >= 1 &&
        data.productId.size() <= 128 &&
        data.productId.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$') &&
        data.modelNumber is string &&
        data.modelNumber.size() >= 1 &&
        data.modelNumber.size() <= 120 &&
        data.title is string &&
        data.title.size() >= 0 &&
        data.title.size() <= 200 &&
        data.category is string &&
        data.category in ['Controllers', 'Motors', 'Machines', 'Robots'] &&
        data.subcategory is string &&
        data.subcategory.size() >= 0 &&
        data.subcategory.size() <= 120 &&
        data.description is string &&
        data.description.size() >= 0 &&
        data.description.size() <= 1500 &&
        data.price is int &&
        data.price >= 0 &&
        data.price <= 1000000000 &&
        data.stockCount is int &&
        data.stockCount >= 0 &&
        data.stockCount <= 100000 &&
        data.stockStatus is string &&
        data.stockStatus in ['In Stock', 'Low Stock', 'Pre-Order'] &&
        data.defaultImageIndex is int &&
        data.defaultImageIndex >= 0 &&
        data.defaultImageIndex <= 3 &&
        data.images is list &&
        data.images.size() >= 1 &&
        data.images.size() <= 4 &&
        data.images[0] is string &&
        data.images[0].size() >= 0 &&
        data.images[0].size() <= 220000 &&
        data.updatedBy is string &&
        data.updatedBy.size() >= 1 &&
        data.updatedBy.size() <= 128 &&
        data.updatedBy.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$') &&
        data.updatedBy == request.auth.uid &&
        data.updatedAt is timestamp &&
        data.updatedAt == request.time;
    }

    // Pillar 2 & Pillar 5: Validation Blueprint for CatalogManifest
    function isValidCatalogManifest(data, docId) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['manifestId', 'customProductIds', 'updatedBy', 'updatedAt']) &&
        data.keys().hasOnly(['manifestId', 'customProductIds', 'updatedBy', 'updatedAt']) &&
        data.manifestId is string &&
        data.manifestId == docId &&
        data.manifestId.size() >= 1 &&
        data.manifestId.size() <= 128 &&
        data.manifestId.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$') &&
        data.customProductIds is list &&
        data.customProductIds.size() <= 500 &&
        (data.customProductIds.size() == 0 || (data.customProductIds[0] is string && data.customProductIds[0].size() <= 128)) &&
        data.updatedBy is string &&
        data.updatedBy.size() >= 1 &&
        data.updatedBy.size() <= 128 &&
        data.updatedBy.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$') &&
        data.updatedBy == request.auth.uid &&
        data.updatedAt is timestamp &&
        data.updatedAt == request.time;
    }

    // Pillar 2 & Pillar 5: Validation Blueprint for CustomerOrder
    function isValidCustomerOrder(data, docId) {
      return data.keys().hasAll([
          'orderId',
          'customerUid',
          'companyName',
          'contactName',
          'email',
          'phone',
          'ntnNumber',
          'strnNumber',
          'city',
          'address',
          'orderNotes',
          'invoiceType',
          'paymentMethod',
          'transactionRef',
          'itemsSummary',
          'subtotalPKR',
          'salesTaxRate',
          'salesTaxPKR',
          'totalPKR',
          'orderStatus',
          'createdAt',
          'updatedAt'
        ]) &&
        data.keys().hasOnly([
          'orderId',
          'customerUid',
          'companyName',
          'contactName',
          'email',
          'phone',
          'ntnNumber',
          'strnNumber',
          'city',
          'address',
          'orderNotes',
          'invoiceType',
          'paymentMethod',
          'transactionRef',
          'itemsSummary',
          'subtotalPKR',
          'salesTaxRate',
          'salesTaxPKR',
          'totalPKR',
          'orderStatus',
          'createdAt',
          'updatedAt'
        ]) &&
        data.orderId is string &&
        data.orderId == docId &&
        data.orderId.size() >= 1 &&
        data.orderId.size() <= 128 &&
        data.orderId.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$') &&
        data.customerUid is string &&
        data.customerUid.size() >= 1 &&
        data.customerUid.size() <= 128 &&
        data.customerUid.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$') &&
        data.companyName is string &&
        data.companyName.size() >= 1 &&
        data.companyName.size() <= 200 &&
        data.contactName is string &&
        data.contactName.size() >= 0 &&
        data.contactName.size() <= 150 &&
        data.email is string &&
        data.email.size() >= 0 &&
        data.email.size() <= 160 &&
        data.phone is string &&
        data.phone.size() >= 5 &&
        data.phone.size() <= 40 &&
        data.ntnNumber is string &&
        data.ntnNumber.size() >= 0 &&
        data.ntnNumber.size() <= 60 &&
        data.strnNumber is string &&
        data.strnNumber.size() >= 0 &&
        data.strnNumber.size() <= 60 &&
        data.city is string &&
        data.city.size() >= 1 &&
        data.city.size() <= 80 &&
        data.address is string &&
        data.address.size() >= 0 &&
        data.address.size() <= 500 &&
        data.orderNotes is string &&
        data.orderNotes.size() >= 0 &&
        data.orderNotes.size() <= 600 &&
        data.invoiceType is string &&
        data.invoiceType in ['1- Cash (Cash on Pick Up)', '2- Online (UBL Bank Transfer)', '3- Sales Tax Invoice (18% GST)', 'Official Tax Invoice (18% GST)', 'Standard Commercial Receipt'] &&
        data.paymentMethod is string &&
        data.paymentMethod in ['1- Cash (Cash on Pick Up)', '2- Online (UBL Bank Transfer)', '3- Sales Tax Invoice (18% GST)', 'Cash on Pick Up (Lahore Facility)', 'Online Bank Transfer (UBL)', 'Sales Tax Invoice (18% GST)'] &&
        data.transactionRef is string &&
        data.transactionRef.size() >= 0 &&
        data.transactionRef.size() <= 120 &&
        data.itemsSummary is string &&
        data.itemsSummary.size() >= 1 &&
        data.itemsSummary.size() <= 4000 &&
        data.subtotalPKR is int &&
        data.subtotalPKR >= 0 &&
        data.subtotalPKR <= 1000000000 &&
        data.salesTaxRate is int &&
        data.salesTaxRate in [0, 18] &&
        data.salesTaxPKR is int &&
        data.salesTaxPKR >= 0 &&
        data.salesTaxPKR <= 200000000 &&
        data.totalPKR is int &&
        data.totalPKR >= 0 &&
        data.totalPKR <= 1200000000 &&
        data.orderStatus is string &&
        data.orderStatus in ['New Order', 'Confirmed', 'Ready for Pick Up', 'Completed', 'Cancelled'] &&
        data.createdAt is timestamp &&
        data.updatedAt is timestamp &&
        data.updatedAt == request.time;
    }

    // Pillar 2 & Pillar 5: Validation Blueprint for OrderManifest
    function isValidOrderManifest(data, docId) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['manifestId', 'orderIds', 'updatedBy', 'updatedAt']) &&
        data.keys().hasOnly(['manifestId', 'orderIds', 'updatedBy', 'updatedAt']) &&
        data.manifestId is string &&
        data.manifestId == docId &&
        data.manifestId.size() >= 1 &&
        data.manifestId.size() <= 128 &&
        data.manifestId.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$') &&
        data.orderIds is list &&
        data.orderIds.size() <= 500 &&
        (data.orderIds.size() == 0 || (data.orderIds[0] is string && data.orderIds[0].size() <= 128)) &&
        data.updatedBy is string &&
        data.updatedBy.size() >= 1 &&
        data.updatedBy.size() <= 128 &&
        data.updatedBy.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$') &&
        (request.auth == null || data.updatedBy == request.auth.uid) &&
        data.updatedAt is timestamp &&
        data.updatedAt == request.time;
    }

    // Pillar 2: Validation Blueprint for AdminUser
    function isValidAdminUser(data, adminUid) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['uid', 'role', 'createdAt']) &&
        data.keys().hasOnly(['uid', 'role', 'createdAt']) &&
        data.uid is string &&
        data.uid == adminUid &&
        data.uid.size() >= 1 &&
        data.uid.size() <= 128 &&
        data.uid.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$') &&
        data.role is string &&
        data.role == 'admin' &&
        data.createdAt is timestamp &&
        data.createdAt == request.time;
    }

    // Collection 1: Live Catalog Inventory (/inventory/{productId})
    match /inventory/{productId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(productId);
      allow list: if false;

      allow create: if isAdmin() &&
        isValidId(productId) &&
        isValidInventoryItem(incoming(), productId);

      allow update: if isAdmin() &&
        isValidId(productId) &&
        isValidInventoryItem(incoming(), productId) &&
        incoming().productId == existing().productId &&
        incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
          'modelNumber',
          'title',
          'category',
          'subcategory',
          'description',
          'price',
          'stockCount',
          'stockStatus',
          'defaultImageIndex',
          'images',
          'updatedBy',
          'updatedAt'
        ]);

      allow delete: if isAdmin() && isValidId(productId);
    }

    // Collection 2: Custom Catalog Manifest (/catalog_index/{manifestId})
    match /catalog_index/{manifestId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(manifestId);
      allow list: if false;

      allow create: if isAdmin() &&
        isValidId(manifestId) &&
        isValidCatalogManifest(incoming(), manifestId);

      allow update: if isAdmin() &&
        isValidId(manifestId) &&
        isValidCatalogManifest(incoming(), manifestId) &&
        incoming().manifestId == existing().manifestId &&
        incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
          'customProductIds',
          'updatedBy',
          'updatedAt'
        ]);

      allow delete: if isAdmin() && isValidId(manifestId);
    }

    // Collection 3: Customer B2B Orders (/orders/{orderId})
    match /orders/{orderId} {
      allow get: if isSignedIn() && isValidId(orderId) && (isAdmin() || existing().customerUid == request.auth.uid);
      allow list: if false;

      allow create: if isValidId(orderId) &&
        isValidCustomerOrder(incoming(), orderId) &&
        (request.auth == null || incoming().customerUid == request.auth.uid) &&
        incoming().createdAt == request.time;

      allow update: if isAdmin() &&
        isValidId(orderId) &&
        isValidCustomerOrder(incoming(), orderId) &&
        incoming().orderId == existing().orderId &&
        incoming().customerUid == existing().customerUid &&
        incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt &&
        incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
          'orderStatus',
          'transactionRef',
          'orderNotes',
          'updatedAt'
        ]);

      allow delete: if isAdmin() && isValidId(orderId);
    }

    // Collection 4: Customer Orders Manifest (/order_index/{manifestId})
    match /order_index/{manifestId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(manifestId);
      allow list: if false;

      allow create: if isValidId(manifestId) &&
        isValidOrderManifest(incoming(), manifestId);

      allow update: if isValidId(manifestId) &&
        isValidOrderManifest(incoming(), manifestId) &&
        incoming().manifestId == existing().manifestId &&
        (isAdmin() || (incoming().orderIds.size() >= existing().orderIds.size() && incoming().orderIds.hasAll(existing().orderIds))) &&
        incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
          'orderIds',
          'updatedBy',
          'updatedAt'
        ]);

      allow delete: if isAdmin() && isValidId(manifestId);
    }

    // Collection 5: Trusted Admins Registry (/admins/{adminUid})
    match /admins/{adminUid} {
      allow get: if isSignedIn() && isValidId(adminUid) && (request.auth.uid == adminUid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if false;

      allow create: if isAdmin() &&
        isValidId(adminUid) &&
        isValidAdminUser(incoming(), adminUid);

      allow update: if false;
      allow delete: if isAdmin() && isValidId(adminUid);
    }
  }
}

{
  "entities": {
    "InventoryItem": {
      "title": "InventoryItem",
      "description": "Live product listing, stock quantity, price, 4-image gallery, and default display image index managed by SZGH Pakistan administrators.",
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "productId": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Unique product identifier.",
          "minLength": 1,

          "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$"

          "description": "Array of up to 4 product image URLs or compressed data URLs."
        },
        "updatedBy": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Firebase Auth UID of the administrator who last updated the listing.",
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 128,
          "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$"
        },
        "updatedAt": {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date-time",
          "description": "Server timestamp of the latest update."
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "productId",
        "modelNumber",
        "title",
        "category",
        "subcategory",
        "description",
        "price",
        "stockCount",
        "stockStatus",
        "defaultImageIndex",
        "images",
        "updatedBy",
        "updatedAt"
      ]
    },
    "CatalogManifest": {
      "title": "CatalogManifest",
      "description": "Manifest of custom product IDs added by administrators so the storefront can subscribe via single-document get without list queries.",
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "manifestId": {
          "type": "string",
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 128,
          "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$"
        },
        "customProductIds": {
          "type": "array",
          "description": "Bounded list of custom product document IDs."
        },
        "updatedBy": {
          "type": "string",

/**
 * @license
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  ArrowRight,
  Play,
  CheckCircle2,
  TrendingUp,
  CornerDownRight,
  ChevronRight,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  HERO_CATEGORIES,
  PRODUCTS_DATABASE,
  VIDEOS_DATABASE,
  SERVICES_DATABASE,
  OFFICIAL_SUBCATEGORY_GROUPS,
  ProductItem,
  ProductCategory,
  VideoPresentation,
  IOBoardOption,
  mergeProductsByModelAndAxes,
  isStandaloneIOBoardProduct,
  normalizeIOBoardModelNumber,
  resolveOfficialSubcategory,
} from './data/products';
import {
  onAuthStateChanged,
  signInWithPopup,
  signOut,
  User,
} from 'firebase/auth';
import {
  doc,
  onSnapshot,
  updateDoc,
  serverTimestamp,
} from 'firebase/firestore';
import {
  auth,
  db,
  googleProvider,
  ADMIN_EMAILS,
  OperationType,
  handleFirestoreError,
} from './lib/firebase';
import { Navbar, NavSection } from './components/Navbar';
import { ProductCard } from './components/ProductCard';
import { ProductDetailModal } from './components/ProductDetailModal';
import {
  CartDrawer,
  CartEntry,
  SubmittedCustomerOrder,
} from './components/CartDrawer';
import { CategoryStoreSubpage } from './components/CategoryStoreSubpage';
import { WhatsAppFloatingChat } from './components/WhatsAppFloatingChat';
import { RetrofitBookingSection } from './components/RetrofitBookingSection';
import { AdminStockModal } from './components/AdminStockModal';

const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = 'sdgh_pakistan_theme_v1';
const CART_STORAGE_KEY = 'sdgh_pakistan_cart_v2';
const METRICS_STORAGE_KEY = 'sdgh_pakistan_metrics_v1';
const ORDERS_STORAGE_KEY = 'sdgh_pakistan_orders_v1';

interface ProductPerformanceMetrics {
  views: number;
  sold: number;
}

const DEFAULT_PERFORMANCE_METRICS: Record<string, ProductPerformanceMetrics> = {
  'SZGH-880TC': { views: 194, sold: 42 },
  'SZGH-CNC1000MDb': { views: 182, sold: 39 },
  'SZGH-CNC1000TDb': { views: 168, sold: 34 },
  'SZGH-130380D': { views: 145, sold: 29 },
  'SZGH-CNC990TDb': { views: 132, sold: 24 },
  'SZGH-09075DC': { views: 118, sold: 21 },
  'SZGH-T450': { views: 96, sold: 9 },
  'SZGH-H1500-B-6': { views: 88, sold: 7 },
};

export default function App() {
  // 1. Persistent Dark / Moderate (Light) Theme State
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<'light' | 'dark'>(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY);
      if (saved === 'dark' || saved === 'light') return saved;
    } catch {
      // Ignore storage access errors in restricted frames
    }
    return 'light';
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    if (theme === 'dark') {
      root.classList.add('dark');
    } else {
      root.classList.remove('dark');
    }
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, theme);
    } catch {
      // Ignore storage errors
    }
  }, [theme]);

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    setTheme((prev) => (prev === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark'));
  };

  // 2. Persistent Shopping Cart State (Supports Controller I/O Board Options)
  const [cart, setCart] = useState<CartEntry[]>(() => {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(CART_STORAGE_KEY);
      if (raw) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed)) return parsed;
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignore corrupt storage
    }
    return [];
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(CART_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(cart));
    } catch {
      // Ignore storage errors
    }
  }, [cart]);

  // Automatic Product Performance Tracking (Views & Sold activity)
  const [performanceMetrics, setPerformanceMetrics] = useState<
    Record<string, ProductPerformanceMetrics>
  >(() => {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(METRICS_STORAGE_KEY);
      if (raw) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') {
          return { ...DEFAULT_PERFORMANCE_METRICS, ...parsed };
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignore storage errors
    }
    return DEFAULT_PERFORMANCE_METRICS;
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(
        METRICS_STORAGE_KEY,
        JSON.stringify(performanceMetrics)
      );
    } catch {
      // Ignore storage errors
    }
  }, [performanceMetrics]);

  const [cartDrawerOpen, setCartDrawerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [activeSection, setActiveSection] = useState<NavSection>('home');

  // 3. Dedicated Category & Subcategory Store Sub-Page State (Opened when clicking any category, subcategory, or Products nav)
  const [activeSubpageCategory, setActiveSubpageCategory] = useState<
    ProductCategory | 'All' | null
  >(null);
  const [activeSubpageSubcategory, setActiveSubpageSubcategory] = useState<
    string | null
  >(null);

  const [selectedProductModal, setSelectedProductModal] =
    useState<ProductItem | null>(null);

  // 5. Interactive Video Modal / Active Video State
  const [activeVideo, setActiveVideo] = useState<VideoPresentation>(VIDEOS_DATABASE[0]);
  const [isPlayingSimulation, setIsPlayingSimulation] = useState(false);

  // 6. Live Firebase Inventory & Admin Auth State
  const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [adminStockModalOpen, setAdminStockModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [customProductIds, setCustomProductIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [cloudInventoryOverrides, setCloudInventoryOverrides] = useState<
    Record<
      string,
      {
        stockCount: number;
        stockStatus: 'In Stock' | 'Low Stock' | 'Pre-Order';
        price: number;
        defaultImageIndex?: number;
        images?: [string, string, string, string];
        modelNumber?: string;
        title?: string;
        category?: ProductCategory;
        subcategory?: string;
        description?: string;
      }
    >
  >({});

  // Submitted Customer Orders State (Local + Live Firestore Sync)
  const [localOrders, setLocalOrders] = useState<SubmittedCustomerOrder[]>(() => {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(ORDERS_STORAGE_KEY);
      if (raw) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed)) return parsed;
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignore storage errors
    }
    return [];
  });
  const [cloudOrderIds, setCloudOrderIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [cloudOrdersMap, setCloudOrdersMap] = useState<
    Record<string, SubmittedCustomerOrder>
  >({});

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(ORDERS_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(localOrders));
    } catch {
      // Ignore storage errors
    }
  }, [localOrders]);

  const isAuthorizedAdmin = useMemo(() => {
    if (!currentUser || !currentUser.emailVerified || !currentUser.email) {
      return false;
    }
    const lowerEmail = currentUser.email.toLowerCase();
    return ADMIN_EMAILS.some((admin) => admin.toLowerCase() === lowerEmail);
  }, [currentUser]);

  const handleGoogleSignIn = async () => {
    try {
      const cred = await signInWithPopup(auth, googleProvider);
      const email = cred.user.email?.toLowerCase() || '';
      const isAdminUser =
        cred.user.emailVerified &&
        ADMIN_EMAILS.some((admin) => admin.toLowerCase() === email);
      if (isAdminUser) {
        setAdminStockModalOpen(true);
      }
    } catch {
      // Popup closed or blocked
    }
  };

  const handleGoogleSignOut = async () => {
    try {
      await signOut(auth);
      setAdminStockModalOpen(false);
    } catch {
      // Ignore sign-out errors
    }
  };

  const handleSecretAdminAccess = async () => {
    if (isAuthorizedAdmin) {
      setAdminStockModalOpen(true);
      return;
    }
    await handleGoogleSignIn();
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribeAuth = onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
      // Keep non-anonymous signed-in users (both customers and admins) active
      if (user && user.isAnonymous) {
        setCurrentUser(null);
        return;
      }
      setCurrentUser(user);
    });
    return () => unsubscribeAuth();
  }, []);

  // Subscribe to Order Manifest (/order_index/active_orders) for Admin
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isAuthorizedAdmin) return;
    const unsubscribeOrderManifest = onSnapshot(
      doc(db, 'order_index', 'active_orders'),
      (snapshot) => {
        if (snapshot.exists()) {
          const data = snapshot.data();
          if (Array.isArray(data.orderIds)) {
            setCloudOrderIds(
              data.orderIds.filter(
                (id: unknown): id is string =>
                  typeof id === 'string' && id.trim().length > 0
              )
            );
          }
        }
      },
      () => {
        // Ignore read error if manifest not created yet
      }
    );
    return () => unsubscribeOrderManifest();
  }, [isAuthorizedAdmin]);

  // Subscribe to individual order documents (/orders/{orderId}) for Admin
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isAuthorizedAdmin || cloudOrderIds.length === 0) return;
    const unsubs = cloudOrderIds.map((orderId) =>
      onSnapshot(
        doc(db, 'orders', orderId),
        (snap) => {
          if (snap.exists()) {
            const d = snap.data();
            setCloudOrdersMap((prev) => ({
              ...prev,
              [orderId]: {
                orderId: d.orderId || orderId,
                customerUid: d.customerUid || '',
                timestamp:
                  d.createdAt && typeof d.createdAt.toDate === 'function'
                    ? d.createdAt.toDate().toLocaleString('en-PK', {
                        dateStyle: 'medium',
                        timeStyle: 'short',
                      })
                    : 'Recent Order',
                companyName: d.companyName || '',
                contactName: d.contactName || '',
                email: d.email || '',
                phone: d.phone || '',
                ntnNumber: d.ntnNumber || '',
                strnNumber: d.strnNumber || '',
                city: d.city || '',
                address: d.address || '',
                orderNotes: d.orderNotes || '',
                invoiceType: d.invoiceType || d.paymentMethod || '1- Cash (Cash on Pick Up)',
                paymentMethod:
                  d.paymentMethod === '2- Online (UBL Bank Transfer)' ||
                  d.paymentMethod === 'Online Bank Transfer (UBL)'
                    ? '2- Online (UBL Bank Transfer)'
                    : d.paymentMethod === '3- Sales Tax Invoice (18% GST)' ||
                        d.paymentMethod === 'Sales Tax Invoice (18% GST)'
                      ? '3- Sales Tax Invoice (18% GST)'
                      : '1- Cash (Cash on Pick Up)',
                transactionRef: d.transactionRef || '',
                itemsSummary: d.itemsSummary || '',
                items: [],
                subtotalPKR: typeof d.subtotalPKR === 'number' ? d.subtotalPKR : 0,
                salesTaxRate: d.salesTaxRate === 0 ? 0 : 18,
                salesTaxPKR: typeof d.salesTaxPKR === 'number' ? d.salesTaxPKR : 0,
                totalPKR: typeof d.totalPKR === 'number' ? d.totalPKR : 0,
                orderStatus: d.orderStatus || 'New Order',
                autoDispatchStatus: 'recorded',
              },
            }));
          }
        },
        () => {
          // Ignore individual doc error
        }
      )
    );
    return () => unsubs.forEach((u) => u());
  }, [isAuthorizedAdmin, cloudOrderIds]);

  const combinedOrders = useMemo(() => {
    const byId = new Map<string, SubmittedCustomerOrder>();
    for (const loc of localOrders) {
      byId.set(loc.orderId, loc);
    }
    for (const [id, cld] of Object.entries(cloudOrdersMap)) {
      byId.set(id, cld);
    }
    return Array.from(byId.values()).reverse();
  }, [localOrders, cloudOrdersMap]);

  const handleOrderSubmitted = (newOrder: SubmittedCustomerOrder) => {
    setLocalOrders((prev) => [...prev, newOrder]);
  };

  const handleUpdateOrderStatus = async (
    orderId: string,
    newStatus: SubmittedCustomerOrder['orderStatus']
  ) => {
    setLocalOrders((prev) =>
      prev.map((o) =>
        o.orderId === orderId ? { ...o, orderStatus: newStatus } : o
      )
    );
    if (isAuthorizedAdmin) {
      try {
        await updateDoc(doc(db, 'orders', orderId), {
          orderStatus: newStatus,
          updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
        });
      } catch {
        // Order may only exist locally
      }
    }
  };

  // Secret Admin Entry Triggers: Ctrl+Shift+A / Alt+Shift+A or URL ?admin=1 / #admin
  useEffect(() => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    if (
      params.get('admin') === '1' ||
      window.location.hash.toLowerCase() === '#admin'
    ) {
      setAdminStockModalOpen(true);
    }

    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.altKey) && e.shiftKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'a') {
        e.preventDefault();
        setAdminStockModalOpen(true);
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, []);

  // Subscribe to custom product manifest (/catalog_index/active_manifest)
  useEffect(() => {
    const manifestPath = 'catalog_index/active_manifest';
    const unsubscribeManifest = onSnapshot(
      doc(db, 'catalog_index', 'active_manifest'),
      (snapshot) => {
        if (snapshot.exists()) {
          const data = snapshot.data();
          if (Array.isArray(data.customProductIds)) {
            const validIds = data.customProductIds.filter(
              (id: unknown): id is string =>
                typeof id === 'string' && id.trim().length > 0
            );
            setCustomProductIds(validIds);
          }
        }
      },
      (error) => {
        handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.GET, manifestPath);
      }
    );
    return () => unsubscribeManifest();
  }, []);

  // Subscribe to all built-in + custom product documents in /inventory/{productId}
  useEffect(() => {
    const allProductIds = Array.from(
      new Set([
        ...PRODUCTS_DATABASE.map((p) => p.id),
        ...customProductIds,
      ])
    );

    const unsubscribes = allProductIds.map((productId) => {
      const path = `inventory/${productId}`;
      return onSnapshot(
        doc(db, 'inventory', productId),
        (snapshot) => {
          if (snapshot.exists()) {
            const data = snapshot.data();
            if (
              typeof data.stockCount === 'number' &&
              typeof data.price === 'number' &&
              (data.stockStatus === 'In Stock' ||
                data.stockStatus === 'Low Stock' ||
                data.stockStatus === 'Pre-Order')
            ) {
              const rawImages = Array.isArray(data.images)
                ? data.images.map((img: unknown) =>
                    typeof img === 'string' ? img : ''
                  )
                : [];
              const normalizedTuple: [string, string, string, string] | undefined =
                rawImages.length > 0
                  ? [
                      rawImages[0] ?? '',
                      rawImages[1] ?? '',
                      rawImages[2] ?? '',
                      rawImages[3] ?? '',
                    ]
                  : undefined;

              setCloudInventoryOverrides((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                [productId]: {
                  stockCount: data.stockCount,
                  stockStatus: data.stockStatus,
                  price: data.price,
                  defaultImageIndex:
                    typeof data.defaultImageIndex === 'number'
                      ? Math.max(0, Math.min(3, Math.round(data.defaultImageIndex)))
                      : undefined,
                  images: normalizedTuple,
                  modelNumber:
                    typeof data.modelNumber === 'string'
                      ? data.modelNumber
                      : undefined,
                  title: typeof data.title === 'string' ? data.title : undefined,
                  category:
                    data.category === 'Controllers' ||
                    data.category === 'Motors' ||
                    data.category === 'Machines' ||
                    data.category === 'Robots'
                      ? data.category
                      : undefined,
                  subcategory:
                    typeof data.subcategory === 'string'
                      ? data.subcategory
                      : undefined,
                  description:
                    typeof data.description === 'string'
                      ? data.description
                      : undefined,
                },
              }));
            }
          }
        },
        (error) => {
          handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.GET, path);
        }
      );
    });

    return () => {
      unsubscribes.forEach((unsub) => unsub());
    };
  }, [customProductIds]);

  const deriveTitleFromDescription = (
    desc?: string,
    fallbackTitle?: string
  ): string => {
    if (fallbackTitle && fallbackTitle.trim().length > 0) {
      return fallbackTitle.trim();
    }
    if (!desc || !desc.trim()) return '';
    const cleaned = desc.trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
    // Take first line or sentence if multi-sentence, up to 140 chars
    const firstSegment = cleaned.split(/\r?\n|(?<=[.!?])\s+/)[0] || cleaned;
    return firstSegment.slice(0, 140).trim();
  };

  const liveProducts = useMemo(() => {
    const builtInIds = new Set(PRODUCTS_DATABASE.map((p) => p.id));

    const mergedBuiltIn = PRODUCTS_DATABASE.map((item) => {
      const override = cloudInventoryOverrides[item.id];
      if (!override) {
        return {
          ...item,
          title: deriveTitleFromDescription(item.description, item.title),
        };
      }
      const hasAnyCustomImage = override.images?.some((u) => u.trim().length > 0);
      const effectiveDesc =
        override.description !== undefined && override.description.trim().length > 0
          ? override.description
          : item.description;
      const effectiveTitle =
        override.title && override.title.trim().length > 0
          ? override.title.trim()
          : deriveTitleFromDescription(effectiveDesc, item.title);

      const resolvedModel = normalizeIOBoardModelNumber(
        override.modelNumber || item.modelNumber,
        effectiveDesc,
        effectiveTitle
      );
      const isIOBoard = isStandaloneIOBoardProduct({
        modelNumber: resolvedModel,
        description: effectiveDesc,
        title: effectiveTitle,
      });

      return {
        ...item,
        modelNumber: resolvedModel,
        title: effectiveTitle,
        category: override.category || item.category,
        subcategory:
          override.subcategory !== undefined
            ? override.subcategory
            : item.subcategory,
        description: effectiveDesc,
        stockCount: override.stockCount,
        stockStatus: override.stockStatus,
        inStock: override.stockCount > 0,
        price: override.price,
        hasIOBoardOption: isIOBoard ? false : item.hasIOBoardOption,
        defaultImageIndex:
          override.defaultImageIndex ?? item.defaultImageIndex ?? 0,
        images: hasAnyCustomImage && override.images ? override.images : item.images,
      };
    });

    const customProducts: ProductItem[] = [];
    for (const customId of customProductIds) {
      if (builtInIds.has(customId)) continue;
      const override = cloudInventoryOverrides[customId];
      if (!override || !override.modelNumber) {
        continue;
      }
      const cat: ProductCategory = override.category || 'Controllers';
      const subcat = override.subcategory ?? '';
      const desc = override.description ?? '';
      const ttl = deriveTitleFromDescription(desc, override.title);
      const normalizedModel = normalizeIOBoardModelNumber(
        override.modelNumber,
        desc,
        ttl
      );
      const isIOBoard = isStandaloneIOBoardProduct({
        modelNumber: normalizedModel,
        description: desc,
        title: ttl,
        price: override.price,
      });

      customProducts.push({
        id: customId,
        modelNumber: normalizedModel,
        title: ttl,
        category: cat,
        subcategory: subcat,
        description: desc,
        features: desc
          ? [
              '1 Year Official SZGH Pakistan Replacement & Technical Support Warranty',
              'Ready Stock Dispatch from Lahore Warehouse Across Pakistan',
            ]
          : [],
        price: override.price,
        usdEstimate: override.price > 0 ? Math.max(1, Math.round(override.price / 278)) : 0,
        inStock: override.stockCount > 0,
        stockCount: override.stockCount,
        stockStatus: override.stockStatus,
        defaultImageIndex: override.defaultImageIndex ?? 0,
        warranty: '1 Year Official SZGH Pakistan Warranty',
        hasIOBoardOption: cat === 'Controllers' && !isIOBoard,
        images: override.images || ['', '', '', ''],
        imageLabels: [
          'Picture #1 View',
          'Picture #2 View',
          'Picture #3 View',
          'Picture #4 View',
        ],
        specs: [
          { label: 'Model Number', value: normalizedModel },
          ...(ttl ? [{ label: 'Product Title', value: ttl }] : []),
          ...(subcat ? [{ label: 'Subcategory', value: subcat }] : []),
          { label: 'Category', value: cat },
          { label: 'Availability', value: `${override.stockStatus} (${override.stockCount} Units)` },
        ],
        compatibility: [],
      });
    }

    return [...customProducts, ...mergedBuiltIn];
  }, [cloudInventoryOverrides, customProductIds]);

  // Merge same model & number of axes into 1 product listing with Axis Selection Tabs
  const mergedCatalogProducts = useMemo(
    () => mergeProductsByModelAndAxes(liveProducts),
    [liveProducts]
  );

  const syncedProductIds = useMemo(
    () => new Set(Object.keys(cloudInventoryOverrides)),
    [cloudInventoryOverrides]
  );

  // Open Dedicated Category Store Sub-Page (optionally pre-filtered to a Subcategory) AND move top section bar to "Products"
  const handleOpenCategorySubpage = (
    category: ProductCategory | 'All',
    subcategory: string | null = null
  ) => {
    setActiveSection('products');
    setActiveSubpageCategory(category);
    setActiveSubpageSubcategory(subcategory);
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  // Track product view and open detail modal
  const handleSelectProduct = (item: ProductItem) => {
    const metricKey = item.baseModelNumber || item.modelNumber;
    setPerformanceMetrics((prev) => {
      const current = prev[metricKey] || { views: 25, sold: 5 };
      return {
        ...prev,
        [metricKey]: {
          ...current,
          views: current.views + 1,
        },
      };
    });
    setSelectedProductModal(item);
  };

  // Cart Handlers with I/O Board Support & Automatic Sold/Demand Tracking
  const handleAddToCart = (
    product: ProductItem,
    quantity = 1,
    selectedIOBoard?: IOBoardOption
  ) => {
    const metricKey = product.baseModelNumber || product.modelNumber.split('-').slice(0, 2).join('-');
    setPerformanceMetrics((prev) => {
      const current = prev[metricKey] || prev[product.modelNumber] || { views: 30, sold: 6 };
      return {
        ...prev,
        [metricKey]: {
          views: current.views + 1,
          sold: current.sold + quantity,
        },
      };
    });

    const cartKey = `${product.id}__${selectedIOBoard ? selectedIOBoard.id : 'base'}`;
    setCart((prev) => {
      const existingIndex = prev.findIndex((item) => item.cartKey === cartKey);
      if (existingIndex > -1) {
        const updated = [...prev];
        updated[existingIndex] = {
          ...updated[existingIndex],
          quantity: updated[existingIndex].quantity + quantity,
        };
        return updated;
      }
      return [
        ...prev,
        {
          cartKey,
          product,
          quantity,
          selectedIOBoard,
        },
      ];
    });
  };

  const handleUpdateCartQuantity = (cartKey: string, newQuantity: number) => {
    if (newQuantity <= 0) {
      handleRemoveCartItem(cartKey);
      return;
    }
    setCart((prev) =>
      prev.map((entry) =>
        entry.cartKey === cartKey ? { ...entry, quantity: newQuantity } : entry
      )
    );
  };

  const handleRemoveCartItem = (cartKey: string) => {
    setCart((prev) => prev.filter((entry) => entry.cartKey !== cartKey));
  };

  const handleClearCart = () => {
    setCart([]);
  };

  const totalCartItems = useMemo(
    () => cart.reduce((sum, entry) => sum + entry.quantity, 0),
    [cart]
  );

  // Automatically rank and pick ONLY the Top 3 Performing, Most Viewed & Most Sold Products for the Home Page
  const topThreePerformingProducts = useMemo(() => {
    const scored = mergedCatalogProducts.map((item) => {
      const key = item.baseModelNumber || item.modelNumber;
      const metrics = performanceMetrics[key] ||
        performanceMetrics[item.modelNumber] || {
          views: 40,
          sold: 8,
        };
      const score = metrics.sold * 5 + metrics.views * 2 + (item.inStock ? 20 : 0);
      return {
        product: item,
        views: metrics.views,
        sold: metrics.sold,
        score,
      };
    });

    scored.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
    return scored.slice(0, 3);
  }, [mergedCatalogProducts, performanceMetrics]);

  const handleTopNavNavigate = (sectionId: NavSection) => {
    setActiveSection(sectionId);
    if (sectionId === 'products') {
      // Open the full paginated catalog (6 products per page) when clicking Products in top bar
      setActiveSubpageCategory((prev) => (prev !== null ? prev : 'All'));
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
      return;
    }

    if (activeSubpageCategory !== null) {
      setActiveSubpageCategory(null);
      setActiveSubpageSubcategory(null);
      setTimeout(() => {
        const el = document.getElementById(sectionId);
        if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
      }, 60);
    } else {
      const el = document.getElementById(sectionId);
      if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col pt-16 sm:pt-20 pb-16 md:pb-0 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 transition-colors duration-150">
      {/* Sticky Navigation Bar */}
      <Navbar
        activeSection={activeSection}
        onNavigate={handleTopNavNavigate}
        onSelectCategorySubcategory={handleOpenCategorySubpage}
        theme={theme}
        onToggleTheme={toggleTheme}
        cartCount={totalCartItems}
        onOpenCart={() => setCartDrawerOpen(true)}
        currentUser={currentUser}
        isAdmin={isAuthorizedAdmin}
        onSignIn={handleGoogleSignIn}
        onSignOut={handleGoogleSignOut}
        onOpenAdminStock={() => setAdminStockModalOpen(true)}
        ordersCount={combinedOrders.length}
      />

      <main className="flex-1">
        {activeSubpageCategory !== null ? (
          /* ================================================================
             DEDICATED PAGINATED CATEGORY STORE SUB-PAGE (6 PRODUCTS PER PAGE)
             (Opened when clicking any Category/Subcategory on Home Page or Products tab)
             ================================================================ */
          <CategoryStoreSubpage
            category={activeSubpageCategory}
            selectedSubcategory={activeSubpageSubcategory}
            onChangeSubcategory={(subcat, targetCat) => {
              setActiveSection('products');
              setActiveSubpageSubcategory(subcat);
              if (targetCat) {
                setActiveSubpageCategory(targetCat);
              }
            }}
            products={mergedCatalogProducts}
            onChangeCategory={(cat) => {
              setActiveSection('products');
              setActiveSubpageCategory(cat);
              setActiveSubpageSubcategory(null);
            }}
            onBackToMain={() => {
              setActiveSubpageCategory(null);
              setActiveSubpageSubcategory(null);
              setActiveSection('home');
              window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
            }}
            onAddToCart={handleAddToCart}
            onSelectProduct={handleSelectProduct}
          />
        ) : (
          /* ================================================================
             MAIN WEBSITE SECTIONS
             ================================================================ */
          <>
            {/* SECTION 1: HOME — RESPONSIVE MOBILE & DESKTOP COMPOSITE WITH 4 CLICKABLE PRODUCT FAMILIES */}
            <section
              id="home"
              className="relative w-full lg:min-h-[calc(100vh-5rem)] flex flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden bg-[#eef2f6] dark:bg-slate-950 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 select-none"
            >
              {/* MOBILE & TABLET HERO (< lg): Clean High-Contrast Banner + Tactile 2×2 Hardware Cards */}
              <div className="lg:hidden flex flex-col w-full">
                {/* Top Mobile Hero Header */}
                <div className="bg-gradient-to-b from-slate-950 via-slate-900 to-[#1E2985] px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 sm:py-8 text-center border-b border-white/10">
                  <p className="text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-semibold tracking-wider text-emerald-400 uppercase">
                    SZGH Pakistan · KH Enterprises (Pvt) Ltd
                  </p>
                  <h1 className="mt-1.5 text-xl sm:text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-white leading-tight text-balance">
                    Total Solution for CNC Systems &amp; Industrial Robots
                  </h1>
                  <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-200 font-medium max-w-xl mx-auto">
                    Tap any of the 4 product families below to open its dedicated store page
                  </p>
                </div>

                {/* 2×2 Touch-Friendly Hardware Category Grid on Mobile */}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 sm:gap-4 p-3 sm:p-5 bg-gradient-to-b from-[#e5ebf1] via-[#f5f7fa] to-[#e2e8f0] dark:from-slate-900 dark:to-slate-950">
                  {HERO_CATEGORIES.map((cat) => (
                    <button
                      key={cat.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleOpenCategorySubpage(cat.id)}
                      title={`Open ${cat.title} Store Page`}
                      aria-label={`Open ${cat.title} Store Page`}
                      className="group flex flex-col justify-between rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200/90 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-xs overflow-hidden text-left transition-transform active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <div className="w-full aspect-4/3 bg-gradient-to-b from-[#eef2f6] to-white dark:from-slate-800 dark:to-slate-900 p-2 sm:p-3 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
                        <img
                          src={cat.image}
                          alt={cat.title}
                          referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                          className="w-full h-full object-contain mix-blend-multiply dark:mix-blend-normal transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-slate-950 text-white flex items-center justify-between gap-1.5 border-t border-slate-800">
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                          <div className="text-[10px] font-mono-tabular text-emerald-400 font-bold truncate">
                            {cat.number}. {cat.title.toUpperCase()}
                          </div>
                          <div className="text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-white truncate">
                            {cat.shortSubtitle}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400 shrink-0" />
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* DESKTOP FULL-WINDOW LANDSCAPE COMPOSITE (lg and up) */}
              <div className="hidden lg:flex lg:flex-col lg:justify-between lg:min-h-[calc(100vh-5rem)] relative w-full">
                {/* Unified Full-Window Landscape Picture Blending All 4 Clickable Product Photos */}
                <div className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full bg-gradient-to-b from-[#e5ebf1] via-[#f5f7fa] to-[#e2e8f0]">
                  <div className="w-full h-full grid grid-cols-4 items-center pt-40 pb-24 px-6">
                    {HERO_CATEGORIES.map((cat) => (
                      <button
                        key={cat.id}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleOpenCategorySubpage(cat.id)}
                        title={`Click to open ${cat.title} Store Page`}
                        aria-label={`Open ${cat.title} Store Page`}
                        className="group relative h-full w-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center overflow-hidden cursor-pointer focus:outline-none"
                      >
                        <img
                          src={cat.image}
                          alt={cat.title}
                          referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                          className="w-full h-full object-contain scale-105 mix-blend-multiply transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-112"
                        />
                        <span className="opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-150 absolute bottom-4 px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-[#1E2985]/95 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-md">
                          Shop {cat.title} →
                        </span>
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>

                  {/* Subtle top and bottom architectural light gradients */}
                  <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 top-0 h-48 bg-gradient-to-b from-slate-950/75 via-slate-950/35 to-transparent" />
                  <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 h-36 bg-gradient-to-t from-slate-950/85 via-slate-950/45 to-transparent" />
                </div>

                {/* Top Brief Writings Overlay */}
                <div className="relative z-10 max-w-[1360px] w-full mx-auto px-8 pt-10 text-center pointer-events-none">
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold tracking-wider text-emerald-300 uppercase">
                    SZGH Pakistan · KH Enterprises (Pvt) Ltd
                  </p>
                  <h1 className="mt-1.5 text-4xl lg:text-5xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-white drop-shadow-sm text-balance">
                    Total Solution for CNC Systems &amp; Industrial Robots
                  </h1>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-base text-slate-100 font-medium max-w-2xl mx-auto drop-shadow-xs">
                    Click any of the 4 product families below to open its dedicated store page
                  </p>
                </div>

                {/* Bottom Clickable Category Strip Aligned Over the 4 Hardware Zones */}
                <div className="relative z-10 w-full border-t border-white/15 bg-slate-950/75 backdrop-blur-xs">
                  <div className="max-w-[1360px] mx-auto px-8 py-3.5 grid grid-cols-4 gap-4 text-left">
                    {HERO_CATEGORIES.map((cat) => (
                      <button
                        key={cat.id}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleOpenCategorySubpage(cat.id)}
                        className="group flex items-center justify-between pr-3 border-r border-white/10 last:border-none text-left cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <div>
                          <div className="text-[11px] font-mono-tabular text-emerald-400 font-semibold">
                            {cat.number}. {cat.title.toUpperCase()}
                          </div>
                          <div className="text-sm font-bold text-white group-hover:text-emerald-300 transition-colors">
                            {cat.shortSubtitle}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 group-hover:text-white transition-colors shrink-0" />
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </section>

            {/* ================================================================
                SECTION 2: HOME PAGE — TOP 3 PERFORMING, VIEWED & SOLD PRODUCTS
                ================================================================ */}
            <section
              id="products"
              className="py-10 sm:py-16 lg:py-20 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800"
            >
              <div className="max-w-[1360px] mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
                {/* Section Header */}
                <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-end justify-between gap-4 sm:gap-6 mb-6 sm:mb-8">
                  <div>
                    <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[#0D5C2B] dark:text-emerald-400 mb-1">
                      <TrendingUp className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                      <span>
                        Automatic Performance Ranking · Most Viewed &amp; Most Sold Hardware
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <h2 className="text-xl sm:text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white tracking-tight">
                      Top 3 Performing Industrial Products
                    </h2>
                    <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1 max-w-2xl">
                      Automatically showcasing our top 3 best-performing, most viewed, and highest-selling systems. Multi-axis models are consolidated into single cards with Axes Selection Tabs.
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleOpenCategorySubpage('All')}
                    className="w-full sm:w-auto inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 sm:py-2.5 rounded-md bg-[#1E2985] hover:bg-[#161f69] dark:bg-emerald-600 dark:hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold transition-colors shadow-xs cursor-pointer shrink-0"
                  >
                    <span>
                      Browse Full Catalog ({mergedCatalogProducts.length} Models · 6 Per Page)
                    </span>
                    <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                  </button>
                </div>

                {/* Official SZGH Product Categories & Subcategories Directory */}
                <div className="mb-10 p-5 sm:p-6 rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-xs">
                  <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 pb-4 mb-5 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#0D5C2B] dark:text-emerald-400">
                        Browse by Main Category &amp; Subcategory
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                        Click any main product family or specific subcategory below to open its dedicated catalog view
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                      {HERO_CATEGORIES.map((cat) => {
                        const count = mergedCatalogProducts.filter(
                          (p) => p.category === cat.id
                        ).length;
                        return (
                          <button
                            key={cat.id}
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleOpenCategorySubpage(cat.id, null)}
                            className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-bold bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-950 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-[#1E2985] hover:text-white dark:hover:bg-emerald-600 transition-colors border border-slate-300/80 dark:border-slate-800 cursor-pointer"
                          >
                            {cat.title} ({count}) →
                          </button>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                    {OFFICIAL_SUBCATEGORY_GROUPS.map((group) => {
                      const groupProducts = mergedCatalogProducts.filter((p) => {
                        if (p.category !== group.category) return false;
                        const resolvedSub = resolveOfficialSubcategory(p);
                        return group.subcategories.some(
                          (s) => s.toLowerCase() === resolvedSub.toLowerCase()
                        );
                      });

                      return (
                        <div
                          key={group.id}
                          className="p-4 rounded-lg bg-slate-50/90 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-200/90 dark:border-slate-800 flex flex-col justify-between"
                        >
                          <div>
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() =>
                                handleOpenCategorySubpage(group.category, null)
                              }
                              className="w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-2.5 mb-2.5 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-left group cursor-pointer"
                            >
                              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <CornerDownRight className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500 shrink-0 stroke-[2.5]" />
                                <span className="text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white group-hover:text-[#1E2985] dark:group-hover:text-emerald-400 transition-colors">
                                  {group.groupTitle}
                                </span>
                              </div>
                              <span className="text-[11px] font-mono-tabular font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                                {groupProducts.length}
                              </span>
                            </button>

                            <ul className="space-y-1.5 pl-1">
                              {group.subcategories.map((subcat) => {
                                const subCount = mergedCatalogProducts.filter(
                                  (p) =>
                                    resolveOfficialSubcategory(p).toLowerCase() ===
                                    subcat.toLowerCase()
                                ).length;
                                return (
                                  <li key={subcat}>
                                    <button
                                      type="button"
                                      onClick={() =>
                                        handleOpenCategorySubpage(
                                          group.category,
                                          subcat
                                        )
                                      }
                                      className="w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-2 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-md text-left text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-[#1E2985] hover:text-white dark:hover:bg-emerald-600 dark:hover:text-white transition-colors group cursor-pointer"
                                    >
                                      <div className="flex items-start gap-1.5 min-w-0">
                                        <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mt-0.5 text-slate-400 group-hover:text-white shrink-0" />
                                        <span className="leading-snug">
                                          {subcat}
                                        </span>
                                      </div>
                                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-tabular px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded bg-slate-200/70 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 group-hover:bg-white/20 group-hover:text-white shrink-0">
                                        {subCount}
                                      </span>
                                    </button>
                                  </li>
                                );
                              })}
                            </ul>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Top 3 Performing Product Cards */}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-7">
                  {topThreePerformingProducts.map((entry, idx) => {
                    const rankLabels = [
                      `#1 Top Performing · ${entry.sold} Sold · ${entry.views} Views`,
                      `#2 Most Viewed & Sold · ${entry.sold} Sold · ${entry.views} Views`,
                      `#3 High-Demand Unit · ${entry.sold} Sold · ${entry.views} Views`,
                    ];
                    return (
                      <ProductCard
                        key={entry.product.id}
                        product={entry.product}
                        rankBadge={rankLabels[idx]}
                        onAddToCart={handleAddToCart}
                        onSelectProduct={handleSelectProduct}
                      />
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>
            </section>

            {/* ================================================================
                SECTION 3: VIDEOS (TECHNICAL OPERATION & RETROFIT DEMOS)
                ================================================================ */}
            <section
              id="videos"

/**
 * Compresses an uploaded image file via HTML5 Canvas into a compact JPEG Data URL (<80KB)
 * so up to 4 product photos fit effortlessly inside a single Firestore document.
 */
async function compressImageFile(file: File): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to read image file.'));
    reader.onload = () => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Invalid image file format.'));
      img.onload = () => {
        const maxWidth = 800;
        const maxHeight = 600;
        let { width, height } = img;

        if (width > maxWidth || height > maxHeight) {
          const ratio = Math.min(maxWidth / width, maxHeight / height);
          width = Math.round(width * ratio);
          height = Math.round(height * ratio);
        }

        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        canvas.width = width;
        canvas.height = height;
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        if (!ctx) {
          reject(new Error('Canvas context unavailable.'));
          return;
        }
        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);

        let quality = 0.75;
        let dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', quality);
        while (dataUrl.length > 180000 && quality > 0.35) {
          quality -= 0.1;
          dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', quality);
        }
        resolve(dataUrl);
      };
      img.src = reader.result as string;
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}

function normalizeCategoryValue(raw: string): ProductCategory | '' {
  const lower = raw.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!lower) return '';
  if (
    lower.includes('controller') ||
    lower.includes('cnc system') ||
    lower.includes('lathe system') ||
    lower.includes('milling system')
  ) {
    return 'Controllers';
  }
  if (
    lower.includes('motor') ||
    lower.includes('servo') ||
    lower.includes('drive') ||
    lower.includes('spindle')
  ) {
    return 'Motors';
  }
  if (
    lower.includes('machine') ||
    lower.includes('vmc') ||
    lower.includes('turning center') ||
    lower.includes('machining center')
  ) {
    return 'Machines';
  }
  if (lower.includes('robot') || lower.includes('arm') || lower.includes('welding')) {
    return 'Robots';
  }
  return '';
}

function extractTitleFromDescription(
  description?: string,
  existingTitle?: string
): string {
  if (existingTitle && existingTitle.trim().length > 0) {
    return stripAxisCountFromText(existingTitle.trim()).slice(0, 200);
  }
  if (!description || !description.trim()) {
    return '';
  }
  const cleaned = stripAxisCountFromText(description).replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
  const firstSegment = cleaned.split(/\r?\n|(?<=[.!?])\s+/)[0] || cleaned;
  return firstSegment.slice(0, 140).trim();
}

  const [statusBanner, setStatusBanner] = useState<{
    type: 'success' | 'error';
    message: string;
  } | null>(null);

  // Local draft edits for existing products in Manage tab
  const [draftEdits, setDraftEdits] = useState<
    Record<
      string,
      {
        stockCount: number;
        price: number;
        stockStatus: 'In Stock' | 'Low Stock' | 'Pre-Order';
        title: string;
        subcategory: string;
        description: string;
      }
    >
  >({});

  // Inline Photo & Default Picture Editor state for existing products
  const [expandedPhotoProductId, setExpandedPhotoProductId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [photoDrafts, setPhotoDrafts] = useState<
    Record<
      string,
      {
        images: [string, string, string, string];
        defaultImageIndex: number;
      }
    >
  >({});

  // New / Loaded Product Listing Form State (All optional tabs left empty by default)
  const [editingCustomProductId, setEditingCustomProductId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [newCategory, setNewCategory] = useState<ProductCategory | ''>('');
  const [newSubcategory, setNewSubcategory] = useState<string>('');
  const [newModelNumber, setNewModelNumber] = useState<string>('');
  const [newTitle, setNewTitle] = useState<string>('');
  const [newPrice, setNewPrice] = useState<string>('');
  const [newStockCount, setNewStockCount] = useState<string>('');
  const [newDescription, setNewDescription] = useState<string>('');
  const [newImages, setNewImages] = useState<[string, string, string, string]>([
    '',
    '',
    '',
    '',
  ]);
  const [newDefaultImageIndex, setNewDefaultImageIndex] = useState<number>(0);
  const [creatingProduct, setCreatingProduct] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Excel (.xlsx) "stock list" Sheet Tracker State
  const [excelFileName, setExcelFileName] = useState<string>('');
  const [excelSheetNames, setExcelSheetNames] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [selectedSheetName, setSelectedSheetName] = useState<string>('');
  const [workbookCache, setWorkbookCache] = useState<XLSX.WorkBook | null>(null);
  const [trackedExcelItems, setTrackedExcelItems] = useState<TrackedExcelStockItem[]>([]);
  const [syncingExcelItems, setSyncingExcelItems] = useState<boolean>(false);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const isAuthorizedAdmin =
    Boolean(currentUser) &&
    currentUser?.emailVerified === true &&
    Boolean(
      currentUser?.email &&
        ADMIN_EMAILS.some(
          (adminEmail) =>
            adminEmail.toLowerCase() === currentUser.email?.toLowerCase()
        )
    );

  const deriveStatusFromCount = (
    qty: number
  ): 'In Stock' | 'Low Stock' | 'Pre-Order' => {
    if (qty <= 0) return 'Pre-Order';
    if (qty <= 3) return 'Low Stock';
    return 'In Stock';
  };

  const handleGoogleLogin = async () => {
    setStatusBanner(null);
    try {
      await signInWithPopup(auth, googleProvider);
    } catch (err) {
      setStatusBanner({
        type: 'error',
        message:
          err instanceof Error
            ? err.message
            : 'Google Sign-In was cancelled or blocked.',
      });
    }
  };

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    setStatusBanner(null);
    await signOut(auth);
  };

  /**
   * Preserves empty image tabs ('') if no image was provided for that slot.
   */
  const buildPreservedImages = (
    rawImages: string[]
  ): [string, string, string, string] => {
    return [
      (rawImages[0]?.trim() || '').slice(0, 215000),
      (rawImages[1]?.trim() || '').slice(0, 215000),
      (rawImages[2]?.trim() || '').slice(0, 215000),
      (rawImages[3]?.trim() || '').slice(0, 215000),
    ];
  };

  const saveProductInventoryToCloud = async (
    product: ProductItem,
    newStockCountVal: number,
    newPriceVal: number,
    explicitStatus?: 'In Stock' | 'Low Stock' | 'Pre-Order',
    customImages?: [string, string, string, string],
    customDefaultIdx?: number,
    customTitle?: string,
    customSubcategory?: string,
    customDescription?: string
  ) => {
    if (!currentUser || !isAuthorizedAdmin) return;

    const clampedStock = Math.max(0, Math.min(100000, Math.round(newStockCountVal)));
    const clampedPrice = Math.max(0, Math.min(1000000000, Math.round(newPriceVal)));
    const computedStatus = explicitStatus || deriveStatusFromCount(clampedStock);
    const imagesToSave = buildPreservedImages(customImages || product.images);
    const defaultIdxToSave = Math.max(
      0,
      Math.min(
        3,
        typeof customDefaultIdx === 'number'
          ? Math.round(customDefaultIdx)
          : typeof product.defaultImageIndex === 'number'
          ? Math.round(product.defaultImageIndex)
          : 0
      )
    );

    const descToSave =
      customDescription !== undefined
        ? customDescription.trim()
        : (product.description || '').trim();
    const rawTitleCandidate =
      customTitle !== undefined ? customTitle.trim() : (product.title || '').trim();
    const titleToSave = extractTitleFromDescription(descToSave, rawTitleCandidate);
    const subcatToSave =
      customSubcategory !== undefined
        ? customSubcategory.trim()
        : (product.subcategory || '').trim();

    setSavingId(product.id);
    setStatusBanner(null);

    const path = `inventory/${product.id}`;
    try {
      await setDoc(doc(db, 'inventory', product.id), {
        productId: product.id.slice(0, 128),
        modelNumber: product.modelNumber.slice(0, 120),
        title: titleToSave.slice(0, 200),
        category: product.category,
        subcategory: subcatToSave.slice(0, 120),
        description: descToSave.slice(0, 1500),
        price: clampedPrice,
        stockCount: clampedStock,
        stockStatus: computedStatus,
        defaultImageIndex: defaultIdxToSave,
        images: imagesToSave,
        updatedBy: currentUser.uid,
        updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
      });

      setDraftEdits((prev) => {
        const next = { ...prev };
        delete next[product.id];
        return next;
      });

      setStatusBanner({
        type: 'success',
        message: `Saved ${product.modelNumber} (${titleToSave || 'No Title'}): Stock = ${clampedStock} (${computedStatus}), Price = ${
          clampedPrice > 0 ? `PKR ${clampedPrice.toLocaleString()}` : 'Empty / Call for Price'
        }`,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      setStatusBanner({
        type: 'error',
        message:
          'Failed to update live product in Firebase. Ensure you are signed in with an authorized admin email.',
      });
      handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.WRITE, path);
    } finally {
      setSavingId(null);
    }
  };

  const handleSyncAllCatalogToCloud = async (forceTitleFromDesc = false) => {
    if (!currentUser || !isAuthorizedAdmin) return;
    setBulkSyncing(true);
    setStatusBanner(null);
    try {
      for (const product of products) {
        const path = `inventory/${product.id}`;
        const computedStatus =
          product.stockStatus || deriveStatusFromCount(product.stockCount);
        const imagesToSave = buildPreservedImages(product.images);
        const defaultIdx = Math.max(
          0,
          Math.min(3, Math.round(product.defaultImageIndex ?? 0))
        );
        const descText = (product.description || '').trim();
        const derivedTitle = forceTitleFromDesc
          ? extractTitleFromDescription(descText) || (product.title || '').trim()
          : extractTitleFromDescription(descText, product.title);

        try {
          await setDoc(doc(db, 'inventory', product.id), {
            productId: product.id.slice(0, 128),
            modelNumber: product.modelNumber.slice(0, 120),
            title: derivedTitle.slice(0, 200),
            category: product.category,
            subcategory: (product.subcategory || '').slice(0, 120),
            description: descText.slice(0, 1500),
            price: Math.max(0, Math.min(1000000000, Math.round(product.price))),
            stockCount: Math.max(0, Math.min(100000, Math.round(product.stockCount))),
            stockStatus: computedStatus,
            defaultImageIndex: defaultIdx,
            images: imagesToSave,
            updatedBy: currentUser.uid,
            updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
          });
        } catch (err) {
          handleFirestoreError(err, OperationType.WRITE, path);
        }
      }

      // Also update any tracked Excel rows in memory so their Title column is populated from Description
      setTrackedExcelItems((prev) =>
        prev.map((row) => ({
          ...row,
          title: forceTitleFromDesc
            ? extractTitleFromDescription(row.description) || row.title
            : extractTitleFromDescription(row.description, row.title),
        }))
      );

      setStatusBanner({
        type: 'success',
        message: forceTitleFromDesc
          ? `Fetched Title from Description and updated all ${products.length} products in Stock Admin & Firebase!`
          : `All ${products.length} catalog products are now synced to Firebase.`,
      });
    } finally {
      setBulkSyncing(false);
    }
  };

  const handleTogglePhotoManager = (product: ProductItem) => {
    if (expandedPhotoProductId === product.id) {
      setExpandedPhotoProductId(null);
      return;
    }
    setExpandedPhotoProductId(product.id);
    if (!photoDrafts[product.id]) {
      setPhotoDrafts((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        [product.id]: {
          images: [...product.images] as [string, string, string, string],
          defaultImageIndex: product.defaultImageIndex ?? 0,
        },
      }));
    }
  };

  const handleExistingProductFileUpload = async (
    product: ProductItem,
    slotIndex: number,
    file: File | undefined
  ) => {
    if (!file) return;
    try {
      const compressedDataUrl = await compressImageFile(file);
      setPhotoDrafts((prev) => {
        const current = prev[product.id] || {
          images: [...product.images] as [string, string, string, string],
          defaultImageIndex: product.defaultImageIndex ?? 0,
        };
        const updatedImages = [...current.images] as [string, string, string, string];
        updatedImages[slotIndex] = compressedDataUrl;
        return {
          ...prev,
          [product.id]: {
            ...current,
            images: updatedImages,
