import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  ShoppingCart,
  Check,
  ShieldCheck,
  ArrowUpRight,
  Cpu,
  Star,
  Layers,
  Flame,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  ProductItem,
  CONTROLLER_IO_BOARD_OPTIONS,
  IOBoardOption,
  AxisVariantOption,
  stripAxisCountFromText,
  isStandaloneIOBoardProduct,
} from '../data/products';

interface ProductCardProps {
  product: ProductItem;
  onAddToCart: (
    product: ProductItem,
    quantity?: number,
    selectedIOBoard?: IOBoardOption
  ) => void;
  onSelectProduct: (product: ProductItem) => void;
  rankBadge?: string;
}

export const ProductCard: React.FC<ProductCardProps> = ({
  product,
  onAddToCart,
  onSelectProduct,
  rankBadge,
}) => {
  const axisVariants: AxisVariantOption[] = product.axisVariants || [];
  const [selectedAxisId, setSelectedAxisId] = useState<string>(
    axisVariants[0]?.id || product.id
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    setSelectedAxisId(product.axisVariants?.[0]?.id || product.id);
  }, [product.id, product.axisVariants]);

  const activeAxis: AxisVariantOption | undefined =
    axisVariants.find((v) => v.id === selectedAxisId) || axisVariants[0];

  // Active images: prefer the active axis variant's custom image if uploaded, else fallback to merged product images
  const activeAxisHasCustomImg = activeAxis?.images?.some(
    (img) =>
      img &&
      img.trim().length > 0 &&
      !img.startsWith('data:image/svg+xml')
  );
  const displayImages: [string, string, string, string] =
    activeAxisHasCustomImg && activeAxis?.images
      ? activeAxis.images
      : product.images;

  const rawDefaultIdx =
    activeAxisHasCustomImg && typeof activeAxis?.defaultImageIndex === 'number'
      ? activeAxis.defaultImageIndex
      : product.defaultImageIndex;

  const defaultIdx =
    typeof rawDefaultIdx === 'number' &&
    rawDefaultIdx >= 0 &&
    rawDefaultIdx < displayImages.length
      ? rawDefaultIdx
      : 0;

  const [selectedImageIndex, setSelectedImageIndex] = useState<number>(defaultIdx);
  const [selectedIOBoardId, setSelectedIOBoardId] = useState<string>(
    CONTROLLER_IO_BOARD_OPTIONS[0].id
  );
  const [justAdded, setJustAdded] = useState<boolean>(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSelectedImageIndex(defaultIdx);
  }, [product.id, selectedAxisId, defaultIdx]);

  const isController =
    (product.category === 'Controllers' || Boolean(product.hasIOBoardOption)) &&
    !isStandaloneIOBoardProduct(product);

  const activeIOBoard: IOBoardOption | undefined = isController
    ? CONTROLLER_IO_BOARD_OPTIONS.find((opt) => opt.id === selectedIOBoardId) ||
      CONTROLLER_IO_BOARD_OPTIONS[0]
    : undefined;

  const basePricePKR = activeAxis ? activeAxis.price : product.price;
  const effectivePricePKR =
    basePricePKR + (activeIOBoard ? activeIOBoard.priceDelta : 0);

  const displayStockCount = activeAxis
    ? activeAxis.stockCount
    : product.stockCount;
  const displayStockStatus =
    (activeAxis ? activeAxis.stockStatus : product.stockStatus) ||
    (displayStockCount > 0 ? 'In Stock' : 'Pre-Order');
  const displayTitle = stripAxisCountFromText(
    activeAxis?.title || product.title || ''
  );
  const displayDescription = stripAxisCountFromText(
    activeAxis?.description || product.description || ''
  );
  const displayModelHeader =
    product.baseModelNumber || product.modelNumber;

  const buildSelectedProductPayload = (): ProductItem => {
    if (!activeAxis) return product;
    return {
      ...product,
      id: activeAxis.id,
      modelNumber: `${displayModelHeader} (${activeAxis.axisLabel})`,
      title: displayTitle || displayModelHeader,
      description: displayDescription,
      price: activeAxis.price,
      stockCount: activeAxis.stockCount,
      stockStatus: activeAxis.stockStatus,
      images: displayImages,
      defaultImageIndex: defaultIdx,
    };
  };

  const handleAdd = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    onAddToCart(buildSelectedProductPayload(), 1, activeIOBoard);
    setJustAdded(true);
    setTimeout(() => setJustAdded(false), 1400);
  };

  return (
    <article
      onClick={() => onSelectProduct(product)}
      className="group flex flex-col bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-lg overflow-hidden transition-all duration-150 hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-[#1E2985] dark:hover:border-emerald-500 cursor-pointer"
    >
      {/* Main Display Image Area */}
      <div className="relative aspect-4/3 w-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-950 overflow-hidden border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-center">
        {displayImages[selectedImageIndex] ||
        displayImages[defaultIdx] ||
        displayImages.find((u) => Boolean(u?.trim())) ? (
          <img
            src={
              displayImages[selectedImageIndex] ||
              displayImages[defaultIdx] ||
              displayImages.find((u) => Boolean(u?.trim()))
            }
            alt={`${displayTitle || displayModelHeader} - ${
              product.imageLabels[selectedImageIndex] || 'Product View'
            }`}
            referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
            className="w-full h-full object-contain p-2 transition-transform duration-200 group-hover:scale-[1.03]"
          />
        ) : (
          <div className="text-xs font-mono-tabular text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-600 select-none">
            {displayModelHeader}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Optional Top Performance / Rank Badge */}
        {rankBadge && (
          <span className="absolute top-3 left-3 inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-amber-500 text-white text-[11px] font-extrabold shadow-sm">
            <Flame className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-current" />
            <span>{rankBadge}</span>
          </span>
        )}

        {/* Click for Details Prompt */}
        <span className="absolute bottom-3 right-3 flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-slate-950/85 text-white text-[11px] font-semibold border border-slate-700 backdrop-blur-xs">
          <span>Details &amp; Features</span>
          <ArrowUpRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* Interactive 4-Thumbnail Gallery Strip */}
      <div
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5 p-2 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/70 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800"
      >
        {displayImages.map((imgUrl, idx) => {
          const hasImage = Boolean(imgUrl && imgUrl.trim().length > 0);
          const isSelected = idx === selectedImageIndex;
          const isDefault = idx === defaultIdx && hasImage;
          return (
            <button
              key={idx}
              type="button"
              disabled={!hasImage}
              onClick={() => hasImage && setSelectedImageIndex(idx)}
              title={
                !hasImage
                  ? 'Empty image slot'
                  : isDefault
                  ? `${product.imageLabels[idx] || `View ${idx + 1}`} (Default Display)`
                  : product.imageLabels[idx] || `View ${idx + 1}`
              }
              aria-label={`View ${product.imageLabels[idx] || `image ${idx + 1}`}`}
              className={`relative aspect-4/3 rounded overflow-hidden border transition-all bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 ${
                !hasImage
                  ? 'border-dashed border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 opacity-40 cursor-default'
                  : isSelected
                  ? 'border-[#1E2985] dark:border-emerald-400 ring-1 ring-[#1E2985] dark:ring-emerald-400 opacity-100 cursor-pointer'
                  : 'border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-800 opacity-65 hover:opacity-100 cursor-pointer'
              }`}
            >
              {hasImage && (
                <img
                  src={imgUrl}
                  alt={`${displayModelHeader} view ${idx + 1}`}
                  referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                  className="w-full h-full object-cover"
                />
              )}
              {isDefault && (
                <span
                  title="Default Display Image"
                  className="absolute top-0.5 right-0.5 w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs"
                >
                  <Star className="w-2.5 h-2.5 fill-current" />
                </span>
              )}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Card Body */}
      <div className="flex flex-col flex-1 p-5 justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="space-y-2.5">
          {/* Category & Stock Line */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            <span className="font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 truncate">
              {product.category}
              {product.subcategory?.trim() ? ` · ${product.subcategory}` : ''}
            </span>
            <span className="font-mono-tabular text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 font-semibold shrink-0">
              {displayStockStatus} ({displayStockCount})
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Model Number */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <div className="font-mono-tabular text-xs font-bold text-[#1E2985] dark:text-sky-400 tracking-wide">
              MODEL: {displayModelHeader}
            </div>
            {activeAxis && (
              <span className="font-mono-tabular text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#1E2985] dark:text-emerald-400">
                {activeAxis.axisLabel}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Product Title */}
          {displayTitle && (
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white leading-snug group-hover:text-[#1E2985] dark:group-hover:text-emerald-400 transition-colors">
              {displayTitle}
            </h3>
          )}

          {/* Brief Description */}
          {displayDescription && (
            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed">
              {displayDescription}
            </p>
          )}

          {/* 1 Year Official Warranty Note */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 pt-1 text-xs font-semibold text-[#0D5C2B] dark:text-emerald-400">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
            <span>Warranty: 1 Year Official SZGH Pakistan Warranty</span>
          </div>

          {/* AXES SELECTION TABS — 3D Button Style with Clear Outline */}
          {axisVariants.length > 0 && (
            <div
              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
              className="mt-2.5 p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100/90 dark:bg-slate-950 border-2 border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 space-y-2 shadow-inner"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Layers className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#1E2985] dark:text-emerald-400" />
                  <span>Select Axes:</span>
                </span>
                {activeAxis && (
                  <span className="font-mono-tabular text-[#1E2985] dark:text-emerald-400">
                    {activeAxis.axisLabel}
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              <div
                role="tablist"
                aria-label="Select number of axes"
                className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 pt-0.5"
              >
                {axisVariants.map((variant) => {
                  const isActiveAxis =
                    (activeAxis?.id || '') === variant.id;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={variant.id}
                      type="button"
                      role="tab"
                      aria-selected={isActiveAxis}
                      onClick={() => setSelectedAxisId(variant.id)}
                      className={`flex-1 min-w-[72px] py-1.5 px-2.5 rounded-md text-xs font-extrabold tracking-wide transition-all duration-150 cursor-pointer select-none ${
                        isActiveAxis
                          ? 'bg-gradient-to-b from-[#2b3ab8] to-[#1E2985] dark:from-emerald-500 dark:to-emerald-700 text-white border-2 border-[#0c1248] dark:border-emerald-200 border-b-4 border-b-[#070b2e] dark:border-b-emerald-950 shadow-[0_3px_6px_rgba(30,41,133,0.35),inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.4)] -translate-y-[1px]'
                          : 'bg-gradient-to-b from-white to-slate-200 dark:from-slate-800 dark:to-slate-900 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 border-2 border-slate-400 dark:border-slate-600 border-b-4 border-b-slate-500 dark:border-b-slate-950 shadow-[0_2px_4px_rgba(15,23,42,0.12),inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.9)] dark:shadow-[0_2px_4px_rgba(0,0,0,0.5),inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.1)] hover:-translate-y-[1px] hover:border-[#1E2985] dark:hover:border-emerald-400 active:translate-y-[1px]'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {variant.axisLabel}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* I/O Board 2-Tab Selector — 3D Button Style with Clear Outline */}
          {isController && (
            <div
              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
              className="mt-2.5 p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100/90 dark:bg-slate-950 border-2 border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 space-y-2 shadow-inner"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Cpu className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#1E2985] dark:text-emerald-400" />
                  <span>I/O Board Option:</span>
                </span>
                {activeIOBoard && activeIOBoard.priceDelta > 0 && (
                  <span className="font-mono-tabular text-[#0D5C2B] dark:text-emerald-400">
                    +PKR {activeIOBoard.priceDelta.toLocaleString()}
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              <div
                role="tablist"
                aria-label="Select I/O Board configuration"
                className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 pt-0.5"
              >
                {CONTROLLER_IO_BOARD_OPTIONS.map((opt) => {
                  const isActiveTab = selectedIOBoardId === opt.id;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={opt.id}
                      type="button"
                      role="tab"
                      aria-selected={isActiveTab}
                      onClick={() => setSelectedIOBoardId(opt.id)}
                      className={`py-1.5 px-2.5 rounded-md text-xs font-extrabold tracking-wide transition-all duration-150 cursor-pointer select-none ${
                        isActiveTab
                          ? 'bg-gradient-to-b from-[#2b3ab8] to-[#1E2985] dark:from-emerald-500 dark:to-emerald-700 text-white border-2 border-[#0c1248] dark:border-emerald-200 border-b-4 border-b-[#070b2e] dark:border-b-emerald-950 shadow-[0_3px_6px_rgba(30,41,133,0.35),inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.4)] -translate-y-[1px]'
                          : 'bg-gradient-to-b from-white to-slate-200 dark:from-slate-800 dark:to-slate-900 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 border-2 border-slate-400 dark:border-slate-600 border-b-4 border-b-slate-500 dark:border-b-slate-950 shadow-[0_2px_4px_rgba(15,23,42,0.12),inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.9)] dark:shadow-[0_2px_4px_rgba(0,0,0,0.5),inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.1)] hover:-translate-y-[1px] hover:border-[#1E2985] dark:hover:border-emerald-400 active:translate-y-[1px]'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {opt.shortLabel}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Price & 3D Add to Cart Footer */}
        <div
          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-3"
        >
          <div>
            <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              {basePricePKR <= 0
                ? 'Pricing'
                : activeIOBoard && activeIOBoard.priceDelta > 0
                ? 'Price (incl. I/O Board)'
                : 'Unit Price'}
            </div>
            <div className="text-lg font-extrabold font-mono-tabular text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
              {basePricePKR > 0
                ? `PKR ${effectivePricePKR.toLocaleString()}`
                : 'Call for Price'}
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleAdd}
            className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-md text-xs font-extrabold transition-all duration-150 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer select-none ${
              justAdded
                ? 'bg-gradient-to-b from-emerald-600 to-[#0D5C2B] text-white border-2 border-emerald-950 border-b-4 border-b-emerald-950 shadow-[0_3px_6px_rgba(13,92,43,0.35),inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.35)]'
                : 'bg-gradient-to-b from-[#2b3ab8] to-[#1E2985] hover:from-[#3344cc] hover:to-[#161f69] dark:from-emerald-500 dark:to-emerald-700 text-white border-2 border-[#0c1248] dark:border-emerald-200 border-b-4 border-b-[#070b2e] dark:border-b-emerald-950 shadow-[0_3px_6px_rgba(30,41,133,0.35),inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.35)] hover:-translate-y-[1px] active:translate-y-[1px]'
            }`}
          >
            {justAdded ? (
              <>
                <Check className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>Added to Cart</span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <ShoppingCart className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>Add to Cart</span>
              </>
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
};


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/ProductDetailModal.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  X,
  ShoppingCart,
  Check,
  ShieldCheck,
  Cpu,
  Layers,
  Plus,
  Minus,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  CheckCircle2,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  ProductItem,
  CONTROLLER_IO_BOARD_OPTIONS,
  IOBoardOption,
  AxisVariantOption,
  stripAxisCountFromText,
  isStandaloneIOBoardProduct,
} from '../data/products';

interface ProductDetailModalProps {
  product: ProductItem | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  onAddToCart: (
    product: ProductItem,
    quantity: number,
    selectedIOBoard?: IOBoardOption
  ) => void;
}

export const ProductDetailModal: React.FC<ProductDetailModalProps> = ({
  product,
  onClose,
  onAddToCart,
}) => {
  const [selectedAxisId, setSelectedAxisId] = useState<string>(
    product?.axisVariants && product.axisVariants.length > 0
      ? product.axisVariants[0].id
      : product?.id || ''
  );

  const activeAxisVariant: AxisVariantOption | undefined =
    product?.axisVariants && product.axisVariants.length > 0
      ? product.axisVariants.find((v) => v.id === selectedAxisId) ||
        product.axisVariants[0]
      : undefined;

  const effectiveImages =
    activeAxisVariant &&
    activeAxisVariant.images &&
    activeAxisVariant.images.some((u) => u && u.trim().length > 0)
      ? activeAxisVariant.images
      : product?.images || ['', '', '', ''];

  const defaultIdx =
    typeof activeAxisVariant?.defaultImageIndex === 'number' &&
    activeAxisVariant.defaultImageIndex >= 0 &&
    activeAxisVariant.defaultImageIndex < effectiveImages.length
      ? activeAxisVariant.defaultImageIndex
      : product &&
        typeof product.defaultImageIndex === 'number' &&
        product.defaultImageIndex >= 0 &&
        product.defaultImageIndex < effectiveImages.length
      ? product.defaultImageIndex
      : 0;

  const [activeImageIdx, setActiveImageIdx] = useState<number>(defaultIdx);
  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState<number>(1);
  const [selectedIOBoardId, setSelectedIOBoardId] = useState<string>(
    CONTROLLER_IO_BOARD_OPTIONS[0].id
  );
  const [addedFeedback, setAddedFeedback] = useState<boolean>(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const initialAxis =
      product?.axisVariants && product.axisVariants.length > 0
        ? product.axisVariants[0]
        : undefined;
    setSelectedAxisId(initialAxis ? initialAxis.id : product?.id || '');

    const nextDefault =
      initialAxis &&
      typeof initialAxis.defaultImageIndex === 'number' &&
      initialAxis.defaultImageIndex >= 0 &&
      initialAxis.defaultImageIndex < 4
        ? initialAxis.defaultImageIndex
        : product &&
          typeof product.defaultImageIndex === 'number' &&
          product.defaultImageIndex >= 0 &&
          product.defaultImageIndex < product.images.length
        ? product.defaultImageIndex
        : 0;
    setActiveImageIdx(nextDefault);
    setQuantity(1);
    setSelectedIOBoardId(CONTROLLER_IO_BOARD_OPTIONS[0].id);
    setAddedFeedback(false);
  }, [product]);

  if (!product) return null;

  const hasAxisTabs =
    Boolean(product.axisVariants) && (product.axisVariants?.length ?? 0) > 0;

  const effectiveModelNumber =
    product.baseModelNumber || product.modelNumber;
  const effectiveTitle = stripAxisCountFromText(
    activeAxisVariant && activeAxisVariant.title.trim().length > 0
      ? activeAxisVariant.title
      : product.title
  );
  const effectiveDescription = stripAxisCountFromText(
    activeAxisVariant && activeAxisVariant.description.trim().length > 0
      ? activeAxisVariant.description
      : product.description
  );
  const effectivePrice = activeAxisVariant
    ? activeAxisVariant.price
    : product.price;
  const effectiveStockCount = activeAxisVariant
    ? activeAxisVariant.stockCount
    : product.stockCount;
  const effectiveStockStatus = activeAxisVariant
    ? activeAxisVariant.stockStatus
    : product.stockStatus;

  const isController =
    (product.category === 'Controllers' || Boolean(product.hasIOBoardOption)) &&
    !isStandaloneIOBoardProduct(product);

  const activeIOBoard: IOBoardOption | undefined = isController
    ? CONTROLLER_IO_BOARD_OPTIONS.find((opt) => opt.id === selectedIOBoardId) ||
      CONTROLLER_IO_BOARD_OPTIONS[0]
    : undefined;

  const unitPricePKR =
    effectivePrice + (activeIOBoard ? activeIOBoard.priceDelta : 0);

  const handleAdd = () => {
    const resolvedProductForCart: ProductItem = activeAxisVariant
      ? {
          ...product,
          id: activeAxisVariant.id,
          modelNumber: `${effectiveModelNumber} (${activeAxisVariant.axisLabel})`,
          title: effectiveTitle || effectiveModelNumber,
          description: effectiveDescription,
          price: activeAxisVariant.price,
          stockCount: activeAxisVariant.stockCount,
          stockStatus: activeAxisVariant.stockStatus,
          images: effectiveImages,
        }
      : product;

    onAddToCart(resolvedProductForCart, quantity, activeIOBoard);
    setAddedFeedback(true);
    setTimeout(() => setAddedFeedback(false), 1500);
  };

  const handlePrevImage = () => {
    setActiveImageIdx((prev) =>
      prev === 0 ? effectiveImages.length - 1 : prev - 1
    );
  };

  const handleNextImage = () => {
    setActiveImageIdx((prev) =>
      prev === effectiveImages.length - 1 ? 0 : prev + 1
    );
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-6 bg-slate-950/80 backdrop-blur-xs overflow-y-auto"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="modal-product-title"
      onClick={onClose}
    >
      <div
        className="relative w-full max-w-5xl bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border-t sm:border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-lg shadow-2xl overflow-hidden sm:my-8 max-h-[92dvh] sm:max-h-none flex flex-col"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* Top Modal Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3.5 sm:px-6 sm:py-4 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 min-w-0 truncate pr-2">
            <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white shrink-0">
              {product.category}
            </span>
            <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
            <span className="truncate hidden sm:inline">{product.subcategory}</span>
            <span aria-hidden="true" className="hidden sm:inline">·</span>
            <span className="font-mono-tabular text-[#1E2985] dark:text-emerald-400 font-semibold truncate">
              {effectiveModelNumber}
            </span>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close product detail view"
            className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-200/60 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Contiguous 2-Column Purchase & Detailed Features Module */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6 sm:gap-8 p-4 sm:p-8 overflow-y-auto flex-1">
          {/* Left Column: Main Image + 4-Thumbnail Switcher + Features (6 cols) */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-6 flex flex-col gap-4 sm:gap-5">
            <div className="relative aspect-4/3 w-full rounded-lg overflow-hidden bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
              {effectiveImages[activeImageIdx] ? (
                <img
                  src={effectiveImages[activeImageIdx]}
                  alt={`${effectiveTitle} - ${product.imageLabels[activeImageIdx]}`}
                  referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                  className="w-full h-full object-contain p-3"
                />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono-tabular">
                  {effectiveModelNumber}
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Prev / Next Navigation Arrows */}
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handlePrevImage}
                aria-label="Previous image"
                className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 p-2 rounded-full bg-slate-900/80 hover:bg-slate-900 text-white border border-slate-700 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleNextImage}
                aria-label="Next image"
                className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 p-2 rounded-full bg-slate-900/80 hover:bg-slate-900 text-white border border-slate-700 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>

              {/* Current View Caption */}
              <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-3 px-3 py-1 rounded bg-slate-950/90 border border-slate-700 text-xs font-mono-tabular text-slate-200">
                View {activeImageIdx + 1}/4: {product.imageLabels[activeImageIdx]}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* 4-Thumbnail Gallery Selector */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2.5">
              {effectiveImages.map((imgUrl, index) => {
                const isActive = index === activeImageIdx;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={index}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setActiveImageIdx(index)}
                    className={`flex flex-col rounded-md overflow-hidden border text-left transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                      isActive
                        ? 'border-[#1E2985] dark:border-emerald-400 ring-2 ring-[#1E2985]/30 dark:ring-emerald-400/30 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800'
                        : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 opacity-75 hover:opacity-100 bg-white dark:bg-slate-950'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="aspect-4/3 w-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-950 overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center">
                      {imgUrl ? (
                        <img
                          src={imgUrl}
                          alt={product.imageLabels[index]}
                          referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                          className="w-full h-full object-cover"
                        />
                      ) : (
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">#{index + 1}</span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <span className="px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 truncate w-full">
                      {index + 1}. {product.imageLabels[index]}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            {/* Detailed Product Features Section */}
            <div className="p-4 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 space-y-2.5">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
                Key Product Features &amp; Capabilities
              </h3>
              <ul className="space-y-2">
                {product.features.map((feat, idx) => (
                  <li
                    key={idx}
                    className="flex items-start gap-2 text-xs text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed"
                  >
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0D5C2B] dark:text-emerald-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                    <span>{feat}</span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Right Column: Detailed Description, Axes Tabs, I/O Board Selector, Specs & Purchase Module (6 cols) */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-6 flex flex-col justify-between gap-6">
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <div className="font-mono-tabular text-xs font-bold text-[#1E2985] dark:text-sky-400 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span>MODEL NO: {effectiveModelNumber}</span>
                  {activeAxisVariant && (
                    <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-[#1E2985]/10 dark:bg-emerald-500/15 text-[#1E2985] dark:text-emerald-300 text-[10px]">
                      {activeAxisVariant.axisLabel}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <h2
                  id="modal-product-title"
                  className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mt-1 leading-tight"
                >
                  {effectiveTitle}
                </h2>
              </div>

              {/* Price & 1 Year Warranty Block */}
              <div className="p-4 rounded-md bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 block">
                    Unit Price (PKR)
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold font-mono-tabular text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                    {unitPricePKR > 0
                      ? `PKR ${unitPricePKR.toLocaleString()}`
                      : 'Call for Price'}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-right">
                  <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-[#0D5C2B] dark:text-emerald-400">
                    <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4" />
                    <span>1 Year Official Warranty</span>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 block mt-0.5">
                    {effectiveStockStatus} ({effectiveStockCount} available)
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Detailed Product Description */}
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">
                  Detailed Product Description
                </h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
                  {effectiveDescription}
                </p>
              </div>

              {/* Axes Selection Tabs — 3D Button Style with Clear Outline */}
              {hasAxisTabs && product.axisVariants && (
                <div className="p-4 rounded-lg bg-slate-100/90 dark:bg-slate-950 border-2 border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 space-y-3 shadow-inner">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <Layers className="w-4 h-4 text-[#1E2985] dark:text-emerald-400" />
                      <span>Select Axes:</span>
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-mono-tabular text-[#1E2985] dark:text-emerald-400">
                      {activeAxisVariant?.axisLabel}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div
                    role="tablist"
                    aria-label="Select controller axes configuration"
                    className="grid grid-cols-3 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2.5"
                  >
                    {product.axisVariants.map((variant) => {
                      const isSelected = activeAxisVariant?.id === variant.id;
                      return (
                        <button
                          key={variant.id}
                          type="button"
                          role="tab"
                          aria-selected={isSelected}
                          onClick={() => setSelectedAxisId(variant.id)}
                          className={`py-2 px-3 rounded-md text-xs font-extrabold tracking-wide transition-all duration-150 cursor-pointer select-none ${
                            isSelected
                              ? 'bg-gradient-to-b from-[#2b3ab8] to-[#1E2985] dark:from-emerald-500 dark:to-emerald-700 text-white border-2 border-[#0c1248] dark:border-emerald-200 border-b-4 border-b-[#070b2e] dark:border-b-emerald-950 shadow-[0_3px_6px_rgba(30,41,133,0.35),inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.4)] -translate-y-[1px]'
                              : 'bg-gradient-to-b from-white to-slate-200 dark:from-slate-800 dark:to-slate-900 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 border-2 border-slate-400 dark:border-slate-600 border-b-4 border-b-slate-500 dark:border-b-slate-950 shadow-[0_2px_4px_rgba(15,23,42,0.12),inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.9)] dark:shadow-[0_2px_4px_rgba(0,0,0,0.5),inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.1)] hover:-translate-y-[1px] hover:border-[#1E2985] dark:hover:border-emerald-400 active:translate-y-[1px]'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {variant.axisLabel}
                        </button>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* I/O Board 2-Tab Selector — 3D Button Style with Clear Outline */}
              {isController && (
                <div className="p-4 rounded-lg bg-slate-100/90 dark:bg-slate-950 border-2 border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 space-y-3 shadow-inner">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <Cpu className="w-4 h-4 text-[#1E2985] dark:text-emerald-400" />
                      <span>I/O Board Configuration:</span>
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-mono-tabular text-[#0D5C2B] dark:text-emerald-400">
                      {activeIOBoard && activeIOBoard.priceDelta > 0
                        ? `+PKR ${activeIOBoard.priceDelta.toLocaleString()}`
                        : 'Standard Included'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div
                    role="tablist"
                    aria-label="Select I/O Board configuration"
                    className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5"
                  >
                    {CONTROLLER_IO_BOARD_OPTIONS.map((opt) => {
                      const isActiveTab = selectedIOBoardId === opt.id;
                      return (
                        <button
                          key={opt.id}
                          type="button"
                          role="tab"
                          aria-selected={isActiveTab}
                          onClick={() => setSelectedIOBoardId(opt.id)}
                          className={`py-2 px-3 rounded-md text-xs font-extrabold tracking-wide transition-all duration-150 cursor-pointer select-none ${
                            isActiveTab
                              ? 'bg-gradient-to-b from-[#2b3ab8] to-[#1E2985] dark:from-emerald-500 dark:to-emerald-700 text-white border-2 border-[#0c1248] dark:border-emerald-200 border-b-4 border-b-[#070b2e] dark:border-b-emerald-950 shadow-[0_3px_6px_rgba(30,41,133,0.35),inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.4)] -translate-y-[1px]'
                              : 'bg-gradient-to-b from-white to-slate-200 dark:from-slate-800 dark:to-slate-900 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 border-2 border-slate-400 dark:border-slate-600 border-b-4 border-b-slate-500 dark:border-b-slate-950 shadow-[0_2px_4px_rgba(15,23,42,0.12),inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.9)] dark:shadow-[0_2px_4px_rgba(0,0,0,0.5),inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.1)] hover:-translate-y-[1px] hover:border-[#1E2985] dark:hover:border-emerald-400 active:translate-y-[1px]'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {opt.shortLabel}
                        </button>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Technical Specifications Table */}
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-2">
                  Technical Specifications
                </h3>
                <div className="border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-md divide-y divide-slate-200 dark:divide-slate-800 text-xs">
                  {product.specs.map((spec, idx) => (
                    <div
                      key={idx}
                      className="grid grid-cols-12 px-3 py-2 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/60"
                    >
                      <span className="col-span-5 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-medium">
                        {spec.label}
                      </span>
                      <span className="col-span-7 font-mono-tabular text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 font-semibold text-right">
                        {spec.label === 'Model Number'
                          ? effectiveModelNumber
                          : spec.value}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Quantity Stepper & Primary 3D Add to Cart CTA */}
            <div className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 space-y-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="flex items-center border-2 border-slate-400 dark:border-slate-600 border-b-4 border-b-slate-500 dark:border-b-slate-950 rounded-md bg-gradient-to-b from-white to-slate-100 dark:from-slate-800 dark:to-slate-900 shadow-xs">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setQuantity((q) => Math.max(1, q - 1))}
                    aria-label="Decrease quantity"
                    className="p-2.5 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <Minus className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                  <span className="px-4 py-2 font-mono-tabular text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                    {quantity}
                  </span>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setQuantity((q) => q + 1)}
                    aria-label="Increase quantity"
                    className="p-2.5 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleAdd}
                  className={`flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-3 px-5 rounded-md text-sm font-extrabold text-white transition-all duration-150 cursor-pointer select-none ${
                    addedFeedback
                      ? 'bg-gradient-to-b from-emerald-600 to-[#0D5C2B] border-2 border-emerald-950 border-b-4 border-b-emerald-950 shadow-[0_3px_6px_rgba(13,92,43,0.35),inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.35)]'
                      : 'bg-gradient-to-b from-[#2b3ab8] to-[#1E2985] hover:from-[#3344cc] hover:to-[#161f69] dark:from-emerald-500 dark:to-emerald-700 border-2 border-[#0c1248] dark:border-emerald-200 border-b-4 border-b-[#070b2e] dark:border-b-emerald-950 shadow-[0_3px_6px_rgba(30,41,133,0.35),inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.35)] hover:-translate-y-[1px] active:translate-y-[1px]'
                  }`}
                >
                  {addedFeedback ? (
                    <>
                      <Check className="w-4 h-4" />
                      <span>Added {quantity} to Cart</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <ShoppingCart className="w-4 h-4" />
                      <span>
                        Add to Cart —{' '}
                        {unitPricePKR > 0
                          ? `PKR ${(unitPricePKR * quantity).toLocaleString()}`
                          : 'Request Quote'}
                      </span>
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/RetrofitBookingSection.tsx

import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  Camera,
  Upload,
  X,
  CheckCircle2,
  Wrench,
  Cpu,
  AlertCircle,
  Image as ImageIcon,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface PhotoSlot {
  slotIndex: number;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  required: boolean;
  fileName?: string;
  previewUrl?: string;
}

const INITIAL_PHOTO_SLOTS: PhotoSlot[] = [
  {
    slotIndex: 0,
    title: '1. Machine Existing Controller',
    subtitle: 'Front operator panel & controller screen',
    required: true,
  },
  {
    slotIndex: 1,
    title: '2. Existing Motor Drives',
    subtitle: 'Electrical cabinet servo & spindle drives',
    required: true,
  },
  {
    slotIndex: 2,
    title: '3. Turret / Tool Post / Tool Changer',
    subtitle: 'Lathe turret, tool post, or VMC ATC magazine',
    required: true,
  },
  {
    slotIndex: 3,
    title: '4. Feed Axis Servo Motors',
    subtitle: 'X / Y / Z axis motor flange & coupling view',
    required: false,
  },
  {
    slotIndex: 4,
    title: '5. Machine Full View / Nameplate',
    subtitle: 'Overall machine bed & manufacturer plate',
    required: false,
  },
  {
    slotIndex: 5,
    title: '6. Additional Cabinet / Wiring View',
    subtitle: 'PLC relays, transformer, or alarm screen',
    required: false,
  },
];

export const RetrofitBookingSection: React.FC = () => {
  const [customerName, setCustomerName] = useState('');
  const [contactNumber, setContactNumber] = useState('');
  const [address, setAddress] = useState('');
  const [serviceType, setServiceType] = useState<'Retrofitting' | 'Maintenance'>(
    'Retrofitting'
  );
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
  const [photoSlots, setPhotoSlots] = useState<PhotoSlot[]>(INITIAL_PHOTO_SLOTS);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState('');
  const [submittedBookingId, setSubmittedBookingId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const fileInputRefs = useRef<(HTMLInputElement | null)[]>([]);
  const bulkInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);

  const uploadedCount = photoSlots.filter((s) => Boolean(s.previewUrl)).length;

  const handleSingleSlotFileChange = (
    slotIndex: number,
    e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>
  ) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
      setErrorMessage('Please upload valid image files (JPG, PNG, WEBP).');
      return;
    }

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => {
      setPhotoSlots((prev) =>
        prev.map((slot) =>
          slot.slotIndex === slotIndex
            ? {
                ...slot,
                fileName: file.name,
                previewUrl: reader.result as string,
              }
            : slot
        )
      );
      setErrorMessage('');
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };

  const handleBulkFilesChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const files = Array.from(e.target.files || []).filter((f) =>
      f.type.startsWith('image/')
    );
    if (files.length === 0) return;

    // Fill empty slots first, up to 6 total capacity
    files.slice(0, 6).forEach((file, idx) => {
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = () => {
        setPhotoSlots((prev) => {
          const next = [...prev];
          // Find first empty slot or fallback to index
          const emptyIdx = next.findIndex((s) => !s.previewUrl);
          const targetIdx = emptyIdx !== -1 ? emptyIdx : idx % 6;
          next[targetIdx] = {
            ...next[targetIdx],
            fileName: file.name,
            previewUrl: reader.result as string,
          };
          return next;
        });
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    });
    setErrorMessage('');
  };

  const handleRemovePhoto = (slotIndex: number) => {
    setPhotoSlots((prev) =>
      prev.map((slot) =>
        slot.slotIndex === slotIndex
          ? { ...slot, fileName: undefined, previewUrl: undefined }
          : slot
      )
    );
    if (fileInputRefs.current[slotIndex]) {
      fileInputRefs.current[slotIndex]!.value = '';
    }
  };

  const buildWhatsAppNotificationUrl = (bookingRef: string) => {
    const attachedPhotosList = photoSlots
      .filter((s) => Boolean(s.previewUrl))
      .map((s) => `• ${s.title} (${s.fileName || 'Attached'})`)
      .join('\n');

    const text = encodeURIComponent(
      `*NEW ${serviceType.toUpperCase()} BOOKING — SZGH PAKISTAN*\n` +
        `----------------------------------\n` +
        `*Booking Ref:* ${bookingRef}\n` +
        `*Service Type:* ${
          serviceType === 'Retrofitting'
            ? 'CNC Machine Retrofitting'
            : 'Machine Maintenance & Repair'
        }\n` +
        `*Customer Name:* ${customerName.trim()}\n` +
        `*Contact No:* ${contactNumber.trim()}\n` +
        `*Factory Address:* ${address.trim()}\n` +
        `*Machine Description:* ${description.trim() || 'Provided in photos'}\n\n` +
        `*Uploaded Machine Photos (${uploadedCount}/6):*\n${attachedPhotosList}\n\n` +
        `*(Note: I am attaching the ${uploadedCount} machine inspection photo(s) in this chat below)*`
    );
    return `https://wa.me/923214800265?text=${text}`;
  };

  const handleSubmitBooking = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!customerName.trim() || !contactNumber.trim() || !address.trim()) {
      setErrorMessage(
        'Please fill in your Name, Contact Number, and Factory/Workshop Address.'
      );
      return;
    }
    if (contactNumber.replace(/\D/g, '').length < 10) {
      setErrorMessage('Please enter a valid 10 or 11-digit phone/WhatsApp number.');
      return;
    }
    if (uploadedCount === 0) {
      setErrorMessage(
        'Please upload at least the required machine photos (Existing Controller, Motor Drives, and Turret/Tool Changer).'
      );
      return;
    }

    setErrorMessage('');
    const bookingRef = `SZGH-BK-${Math.floor(10000 + Math.random() * 90000)}`;
    setSubmittedBookingId(bookingRef);

    // Immediately redirect/open WhatsApp notification to +923214800265 with full booking details
    window.location.href = buildWhatsAppNotificationUrl(bookingRef);
  };

  const handleResetBooking = () => {
    setSubmittedBookingId(null);
    setCustomerName('');
    setContactNumber('');
    setAddress('');
    setDescription('');
    setPhotoSlots(INITIAL_PHOTO_SLOTS);
  };

  const whatsappBookingSummaryHref = () => {
    return buildWhatsAppNotificationUrl(submittedBookingId || 'SZGH-BK');
  };

  return (
    <section
      id="application"
      className="py-16 lg:py-20 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950"
    >
      <div className="max-w-[1360px] mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        {/* Section Header */}
        <div className="mb-10">
          <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#0D5C2B] dark:text-emerald-400 mb-1">
            On-Site Engineering Assessment · SZGH Pakistan (KH Enterprises Pvt Ltd)
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white tracking-tight">
            CNC Retrofit &amp; Machine Maintenance Booking
          </h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1 max-w-2xl">
            Book our field engineering team for a complete CNC retrofit overhaul or emergency machine maintenance. Upload up to 6 photos of your existing machine components so our engineers arrive with the exact matching controller, drives, and adapter plates.
          </p>
        </div>

        {submittedBookingId ? (
          /* Booking Confirmation State */
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-lg p-6 sm:p-10 max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-6">
            <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
              <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950/70 text-[#0D5C2B] dark:text-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-7 h-7" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="text-xs font-mono-tabular font-bold text-[#0D5C2B] dark:text-emerald-400">
                  BOOKING REFERENCE #{submittedBookingId}
                </div>
                <h3 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mt-0.5">
                  {serviceType === 'Retrofitting'
                    ? 'CNC Machine Retrofitting Booking Received'
                    : 'Machine Maintenance Booking Received'}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mt-1">
                  Thank you, <strong>{customerName}</strong>. Our senior CNC retrofit engineer will review your {uploadedCount} uploaded machine photo(s) and contact you at <strong className="font-mono-tabular">{contactNumber}</strong> shortly.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Uploaded Photos Preview in Receipt */}
            <div className="p-4 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 space-y-3">
              <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                Attached Machine Inspection Photos ({uploadedCount} / 6):
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-6 gap-3">
                {photoSlots
                  .filter((s) => Boolean(s.previewUrl))
                  .map((slot) => (
                    <div
                      key={slot.slotIndex}
                      className="rounded border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 overflow-hidden bg-white dark:bg-slate-900"
                    >
                      <img
                        src={slot.previewUrl}
                        alt={slot.title}
                        className="w-full h-20 object-cover"
                      />
                      <div className="p-1.5 text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 truncate">
                        {slot.title}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 pt-2">
              <a
                href={whatsappBookingSummaryHref()}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-md bg-[#25D366] hover:bg-[#20bd5a] text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold transition-colors"
              >
                Send Booking Summary on WhatsApp (+92 321 4800265)
              </a>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleResetBooking}
                className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-md border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                Submit Another Machine Booking
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* Booking Form */
          <form
            onSubmit={handleSubmitBooking}
            className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 items-start"
          >
            {/* Left 5 Cols: Customer Details, Service Selection & Description */}
            <div className="lg:col-span-5 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-lg p-6 sm:p-7 space-y-5">
              <h3 className="text-base font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 pb-3">
                1. Customer &amp; Service Details
              </h3>

              {errorMessage && (
                <div className="p-3 rounded-md bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/60 border border-red-300 dark:border-red-800 flex items-start gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-red-700 dark:text-red-300">
                  <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                  <span>{errorMessage}</span>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Service Option Choice: Retrofitting vs Maintenance */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
                  Select Required Service *
                </label>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setServiceType('Retrofitting')}
                    className={`flex flex-col items-start p-3.5 rounded-lg border text-left transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                      serviceType === 'Retrofitting'
                        ? 'border-[#1E2985] dark:border-emerald-400 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 ring-2 ring-[#1E2985]/20 dark:ring-emerald-400/20'
                        : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-950/50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <Cpu className="w-5 h-5 text-[#1E2985] dark:text-emerald-400 mb-1.5" />
                    <span className="text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                      CNC Retrofitting
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                      New controller, servos &amp; cabinet upgrade
                    </span>
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setServiceType('Maintenance')}
                    className={`flex flex-col items-start p-3.5 rounded-lg border text-left transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                      serviceType === 'Maintenance'
                        ? 'border-[#1E2985] dark:border-emerald-400 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 ring-2 ring-[#1E2985]/20 dark:ring-emerald-400/20'
                        : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-950/50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <Wrench className="w-5 h-5 text-[#0D5C2B] dark:text-emerald-400 mb-1.5" />
                    <span className="text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                      Machine Maintenance
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                      Fault diagnostics, drive &amp; turret repair
                    </span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Customer Name */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1">
                  Customer / Engineer Name *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={customerName}
                  onChange={(e) => setCustomerName(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Enter your full name"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-md border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-[#1E2985] dark:focus:border-emerald-400"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Contact Number */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1">
                  Contact Number (Phone / WhatsApp) *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="tel"
                  required
                  value={contactNumber}
                  onChange={(e) => setContactNumber(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g., 0321-4800265"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-md border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 text-sm font-mono-tabular text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-[#1E2985] dark:focus:border-emerald-400"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Factory / Workshop Address */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1">
                  Factory / Workshop Address &amp; City *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={address}
                  onChange={(e) => setAddress(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Plot #, Industrial Estate, City (e.g., Lahore, Sialkot, Gujranwala)"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-md border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-[#1E2985] dark:focus:border-emerald-400"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Description Box */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1">
                  Machine Description &amp; Issue / Requirement
                </label>
                <textarea
                  rows={4}
                  value={description}
                  onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Describe your machine model, number of axes, existing controller brand, turret/tool changer type, or current fault..."
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-md border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-[#1E2985] dark:focus:border-emerald-400"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Right 7 Cols: 6-Photo Machine Inspection Uploading Section with Instructions */}
            <div className="lg:col-span-7 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-lg p-6 sm:p-7 space-y-6">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 pb-3">
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-base font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                    2. Upload Machine Photos (6-Photo Capacity)
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    Click any slot below or use bulk upload to attach up to 6 clear photos
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <span className="font-mono-tabular text-xs font-bold text-[#1E2985] dark:text-emerald-400">
                    {uploadedCount} / 6 Uploaded
                  </span>
                  <input
                    ref={bulkInputRef}
                    type="file"
                    accept="image/*"
                    multiple
                    onChange={handleBulkFilesChange}
                    className="hidden"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => bulkInputRef.current?.click()}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0D5C2B] dark:text-emerald-400" />
                    <span>Select Multiple Photos</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Mandatory Photo Instructions Box */}
              <div className="p-4 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 space-y-2">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                  <Camera className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0D5C2B] dark:text-emerald-400" />
                  <span>Photo Must Include:</span>
                </div>
                <ul className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-xs text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 font-semibold pt-1">
                  <li className="p-2 rounded bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
                    1- Machine existing Controller
                  </li>
                  <li className="p-2 rounded bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
                    2- Existing motor drives
                  </li>
                  <li className="p-2 rounded bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
                    3- Turret / Tool post / Tool Changer
                  </li>
                </ul>
              </div>

              {/* 6-Slot Photo Upload Grid */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                {photoSlots.map((slot) => {
                  const hasPhoto = Boolean(slot.previewUrl);
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={slot.slotIndex}
                      className={`relative flex flex-col justify-between rounded-lg border overflow-hidden transition-colors ${
                        hasPhoto
                          ? 'border-[#0D5C2B] dark:border-emerald-500 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950'
                          : slot.required
                          ? 'border-dashed border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50/60 dark:bg-slate-950/60 hover:border-[#1E2985] dark:hover:border-emerald-400'
                          : 'border-dashed border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50/30 dark:bg-slate-950/30 hover:border-slate-400'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <input
                        ref={(el) => {
                          fileInputRefs.current[slot.slotIndex] = el;
                        }}
                        type="file"
                        accept="image/*"
                        onChange={(e) => handleSingleSlotFileChange(slot.slotIndex, e)}
                        className="hidden"
                      />

                      {hasPhoto ? (
                        <div className="relative aspect-4/3 w-full bg-slate-950">
                          <img
                            src={slot.previewUrl}
                            alt={slot.title}
                            className="w-full h-full object-cover"
                          />
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleRemovePhoto(slot.slotIndex)}
                            aria-label={`Remove ${slot.title}`}
                            className="absolute top-2 right-2 p-1 rounded-full bg-slate-950/80 hover:bg-red-600 text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                          >
                            <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      ) : (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => fileInputRefs.current[slot.slotIndex]?.click()}
                          className="aspect-4/3 w-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 text-center cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          <ImageIcon className="w-7 h-7 text-slate-400 mb-2" />
                          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[#1E2985] dark:text-emerald-400">
                            + Upload Photo
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1">
                            {slot.subtitle}
                          </span>
                        </button>
                      )}

                      <div className="p-2.5 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 flex items-center justify-between gap-1">
                        <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 truncate">
                          {slot.title}
                        </span>
                        {slot.required && !hasPhoto && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 shrink-0">
                            Required
                          </span>
                        )}
                        {hasPhoto && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#0D5C2B] dark:text-emerald-400 shrink-0">
                            Ready
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                className="w-full py-3.5 px-6 rounded-md bg-[#1E2985] hover:bg-[#151d63] dark:bg-emerald-600 dark:hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-sm font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-xs"
              >
                Submit Form
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

import React from 'react';

interface WhatsAppFloatingChatProps {
  whatsappNumber?: string;
  contextLabel?: string;
}

export const WhatsAppFloatingChat: React.FC<WhatsAppFloatingChatProps> = ({
  whatsappNumber = '+923214800265',
  contextLabel,
}) => {
  const cleanPhone = whatsappNumber.replace(/\D/g, '');
  const prefix = contextLabel ? `[${contextLabel}] ` : '';
  const defaultText = encodeURIComponent(
    `Assalam-o-Alaikum SZGH Pakistan (KH Enterprises),\n${prefix}I would like to inquire about your CNC products and services.`
  );
  const whatsappHref = `https://wa.me/${cleanPhone}?text=${defaultText}`;

  return (
    <a
      href={whatsappHref}
      target="_blank"
      rel="noopener noreferrer"
      aria-label="Chat on WhatsApp with SZGH Pakistan (+92 321 4800265)"
      title="Chat on WhatsApp (+92 321 4800265)"
      className="fixed bottom-20 right-4 md:bottom-6 md:right-6 z-40 w-12 h-12 sm:w-14 sm:h-14 rounded-full bg-[#25D366] hover:bg-[#20bd5a] text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-xl transition-transform duration-150 hover:scale-110 active:scale-95 cursor-pointer"
    >
      {/* Official WhatsApp Vector Logo */}
      <svg
        xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
        viewBox="0 0 32 32"
        className="w-7 h-7 sm:w-8 sm:h-8 fill-current"
        aria-hidden="true"
      >
        <path d="M16.004 3.2c-7.072 0-12.8 5.728-12.8 12.8 0 2.256.592 4.464 1.712 6.416L3.2 28.8l6.56-1.712c1.888 1.024 4.016 1.568 6.24 1.568h.004c7.072 0 12.8-5.728 12.8-12.8 0-3.424-1.328-6.64-3.744-9.056C22.644 4.528 19.428 3.2 16.004 3.2zm0 23.296c-1.92 0-3.808-.512-5.456-1.488l-.384-.224-4.048 1.056 1.072-3.952-.256-.4c-1.072-1.712-1.648-3.68-1.648-5.712 0-5.904 4.816-10.72 10.724-10.72 2.864 0 5.552 1.12 7.584 3.136 2.016 2.032 3.136 4.72 3.136 7.584 0 5.904-4.816 10.72-10.724 10.72zm5.872-8.016c-.32-.16-1.904-.944-2.192-1.04-.288-.112-.512-.16-.72.16-.208.32-.832 1.04-1.024 1.264-.192.208-.368.24-.688.08-.32-.16-1.36-.5-2.592-1.6-.96-.848-1.6-1.888-1.792-2.208-.192-.32-.016-.496.144-.656.144-.144.32-.368.48-.56.16-.192.208-.32.32-.528.112-.208.048-.4-.032-.56-.08-.16-.72-1.744-.992-2.384-.256-.624-.528-.544-.72-.544-.192-.016-.4-.016-.608-.016-.208 0-.56.08-.848.4-.288.32-1.12 1.088-1.12 2.656s1.152 3.088 1.312 3.296c.16.208 2.256 3.44 5.472 4.832.768.336 1.36.528 1.824.672.768.24 1.472.208 2.032.128.624-.096 1.904-.784 2.176-1.536.272-.752.272-1.392.192-1.536-.08-.144-.288-.224-.608-.384z" />
      </svg>
    </a>
  );
};

import { initializeApp } from 'firebase/app';
import { getAuth, GoogleAuthProvider } from 'firebase/auth';
import {
  getFirestore,
  doc,
  getDocFromServer,
} from 'firebase/firestore';
import firebaseConfig from '../../firebase-applet-config.json';

const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
export const db = getFirestore(app, firebaseConfig.firestoreDatabaseId);
export const auth = getAuth(app);
export const googleProvider = new GoogleAuthProvider();

export const ADMIN_EMAILS = ['khubaib4000@gmail.com', 'grnoman786@gmail.com'];

export enum OperationType {
  CREATE = 'create',
  UPDATE = 'update',
  DELETE = 'delete',
  LIST = 'list',
  GET = 'get',
  WRITE = 'write',
}

export interface FirestoreErrorInfo {
  error: string;
  operationType: OperationType;
  path: string | null;
  authInfo: {
    userId?: string | null;
    email?: string | null;
    emailVerified?: boolean | null;
    isAnonymous?: boolean | null;
    tenantId?: string | null;
    providerInfo?: {
      providerId?: string | null;
      email?: string | null;
    }[];
  };
}

export function handleFirestoreError(
  error: unknown,
  operationType: OperationType,
  path: string | null
): never {
  const errInfo: FirestoreErrorInfo = {
    error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
    authInfo: {
      userId: auth.currentUser?.uid,
      email: auth.currentUser?.email,
      emailVerified: auth.currentUser?.emailVerified,
      isAnonymous: auth.currentUser?.isAnonymous,
      tenantId: auth.currentUser?.tenantId,
      providerInfo:
        auth.currentUser?.providerData?.map((provider) => ({
          providerId: provider.providerId,
          email: provider.email,
        })) || [],
    },
    operationType,
    path,
  };
  console.error('Firestore Error: ', JSON.stringify(errInfo));
  throw new Error(JSON.stringify(errInfo));
}

// Validate connection to Firestore on boot
async function testConnection() {
  try {
    await getDocFromServer(doc(db, 'inventory', 'connection_check'));
  } catch (error) {
    if (
      error instanceof Error &&
      error.message.includes('the client is offline')
    ) {
      console.error('Please check your Firebase configuration.');
    }
  }
}

testConnection();

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * SZGH PAKISTAN (BY KH ENTERPRISES PVT LTD) — PRODUCT & CONTENT DATABASE
 * ============================================================================
 *
 * Supports the 4 core Hero Picture Categories:
 * 1. 'Controllers' — CNC Lathe, Milling & Multi-Axis Controllers (Includes I/O Board Selector)
 * 2. 'Motors'      — AC Servo Motors, Digital Servo Drives & Spindle Servo Motors
 * 3. 'Machines'    — Complete CNC Turning Centers & Vertical Machining Centers (VMCs)
 * 4. 'Robots'      — 6-Axis Industrial Welding, Handling, Grinding & Spraying Robots
 */

export type ProductCategory = 'Controllers' | 'Motors' | 'Machines' | 'Robots';

export interface IOBoardOption {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  shortLabel: string;
  priceDelta: number; // Additional PKR price when selected
}

  },

export interface ProductSpec {
  label: string;
  value: string;
}

export interface AxisVariantOption {
  id: string;
  axisLabel: string; // e.g., '2-Axis', '3-Axis', '4-Axis', '5-Axis'
  axisCount: number; // e.g., 2, 3, 4, 5
  modelNumber: string; // Specific model number for this axis option
  title: string;
  description: string;
  price: number;
  stockCount: number;
  stockStatus?: 'In Stock' | 'Low Stock' | 'Pre-Order';
  images?: [string, string, string, string];
  defaultImageIndex?: number;
}

export interface ProductItem {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  modelNumber: string;
  baseModelNumber?: string;
  axisVariants?: AxisVariantOption[];
  category: ProductCategory;
  subcategory: string;
  description: string;
  features: string[];
  price: number; // Base price in PKR
  usdEstimate: number;
  inStock: boolean;
  stockCount: number;
  stockStatus?: 'In Stock' | 'Low Stock' | 'Pre-Order';
  defaultImageIndex?: number; // 0..3 index of the highlighted default image
  warranty: string; // Standard 1 Year Warranty Note
  hasIOBoardOption?: boolean; // True ONLY for Controllers
  images: [string, string, string, string];
  imageLabels: [string, string, string, string];
  specs: ProductSpec[];
  compatibility: string[];
}

/**
